--- Session 13 (2026-04-29 evening — late) — Dashboard polish + watch typeahead + manager kanban + receipt PDFs + NUMERIC cleanup + test scaffold ---

 169. PHASE 8 — DASHBOARD CHARTS WIRED TO REAL DATA. The 3 charts that
     were on demoBars/demoSeries PRNG are now live. dashboard/page.tsx
     adds 4 new aggregations folded into the existing
     dailyRevenueWindowStart 14-day window: pawnsCreated14d (loans
     COUNT by created_at day), redemptions14d (loan_events COUNT by
     occurred_at where event_type='redemption' — append-only, so the
     date is preserved across edits, unlike loans.updated_at),
     layawayPayments14d (layaway_payments SUM where amount > 0 —
     refunds excluded so chart shows gross collections), and
     repairsCompleted14d (repair_tickets COUNT where completed_at IS
     NOT NULL — covers markCompleteAction → ready and approveQaAction
     → ready). dashboard/content.tsx LayawaysPanel / PawnPanel /
     RepairsPanel consume real props with sane empty-tenant zero-fill;
     each chart axis caps at seriesMax * 1.1 with minima for empty
     series. demoBars + demoSeries removed entirely. Sparkline on the
     OverviewPanel module cards is left unchanged (separate visual
     element, not in scope). Commit pending.

 170. WATCH-MODELS TYPEAHEAD — LIVE INSIDE InlinePawnCalculator. New
     `WatchLookupSection` mounts above the LTV row in
     components/pawn/InlinePawnCalculator.tsx. Debounced search
     (200ms, AbortController-cancelled, onChange-handler-driven per
     the project gotcha) hits /api/watch-models/search, lists matches
     as the user types, and on selection displays a card with brand /
     model / ref / year-range plus Min/Mid/Max stat tiles and a
     copy-to-clipboard button for the descriptive text. Read-only by
     design — CollateralItemsList rows are React-controlled, so DOM
     writes via the native-setter shim would be fragile and tightly
     couple the lookup to internal form state. Bilingual disclaimer
     locked: "wholesale floors — anchor only, not a formal appraisal."
     i18n keys at pawn.inlineCalc.watchLookup.* in both en.ts +
     es.ts. Resolves the open Session-11 polish note (item 164).
     Future enhancement on backlog: imperative-handle integration to
     auto-fill row description + est_value when the operator selects
     a model. Commit pending.

 171. MANAGER REPAIR KANBAN — /repair/board (NEW). New route at
     app/(staff)/repair/board/{page,content}.tsx. Manager-only role
     gate (owner/manager/chain_admin); non-managers redirect to
     /repair (NOT /no-tenant — they keep their normal access).
     Module-gated on has_repair. Five status columns in workflow
     order: assigned → in_progress → needs_parts → tech_qa → ready.
     Terminal states excluded — list page handles those. Sort within
     column: promised_date asc nulls-last, then created_at asc.
     Tech chip strip with active-count badges, sorted by load
     (count desc, then alphabetic). Service-type filter. Cards show
     ticket number, customer "Last, First", service-type badge,
     title (line-clamp-2), assigned tech (or "Unassigned"), and a
     due-date column with overdue/due-soon coloring. Click-through
     to /repair/[id]. Responsive grid: 1 / 3 / 5 cols across
     breakpoints. /repair list gets an "Open work-order board"
     button visible only to managers (isManager prop threads down
     from page.tsx). i18n keys at repair.board.* + repair.list
     .openBoard. Resolves backlog item #24 (Session 12 added the
     data + workflow; Session 13 ships the view). Commit pending.

 172. PRE-EXISTING SET-STATE-IN-EFFECT LINT ERRORS FIXED. buy/new/
     form.tsx had two offending useEffects: one syncing items[].
     payout from melt-value computation (driven off a joined item
     signature), and one repopulating scalars from validation echo.
     Both rewrote as compute-during-render per the Session 8
     pattern: signature-tracking sentinel state + conditional
     setStates outside any effect body. customers/new/form.tsx had
     one useEffect for validation echo — same pattern. Removed
     unused `useEffect` imports from both files. Net: -3 errors
     against react-hooks/set-state-in-effect (the 5 reported by
     lint at start of session were 3 from these files + 2
     pre-existing react/no-unescaped-entities in admin/billing/
     content.tsx which are NOT mine and stay open). Commit pending.

 173. CROSS-MODULE ACTIVITY FEED — DASHBOARD OVERVIEW. dashboard/
     page.tsx queries 6 module sources in parallel (customers,
     inventory_items, loans gated by has_pawn, sales + layaways
     gated by has_retail, repair_tickets gated by has_repair),
     6 most-recent rows per source, merged into a unified top-10
     timeline sorted by created_at desc. Each row carries kind
     ('customer' | 'inventory' | 'pawn' | 'sale' | 'layaway' |
     'repair'), title (customer name OR item description), subtitle
     (ticket / sale / layaway number), amount (principal / total /
     list_price / quote_amount / null), and href (drill-down URL).
     dashboard/content.tsx ActivityRow component renders kind-
     tagged feed items with click-through to /customers/[id],
     /inventory/[id], /pawn/[id], /pos/sales/[id], /pos/layaways/
     [id], or /repair/[id]. Replaces the recentCustomers +
     recentItems split feed. Tag tones reuse the dashboard's
     per-module CSS variables (--m-pawn-text / --m-sales-text /
     --m-layaway-text / --m-repair-text). Commit pending.

 174. POS RECEIPT PDF — BILINGUAL EN+ES. Three new files. lib/pdf/
     SaleReceiptPDF.tsx (component): Letter paper, header with
     tenant + sale number + status pill, customer + sale-meta block
     (customer name OR "Anonymous walk-in" / phone / type / tax
     rate), items table (description / SKU / qty / unit / line
     total), totals breakdown (subtotal / discount only when > 0 /
     tax / grand total), payments list with grand-total + balance-
     due if nonzero, optional notes, return-policy block, footer
     with page X of Y. Stacked-pair labels for bilingual rows.
     lib/pdf/render-sale-receipt.tsx: server-side renderer pulls
     sale + customer (single embedded select) + tenant + sale_items
     with inventory.sku join + sale_payments via user-scoped
     supabase (RLS enforces tenant). app/api/print/sale/[id]/
     route.ts: gates getCtx → tenant lookup → requireRoleInTenant
     (['owner','manager','pawn_clerk','chain_admin']) → render →
     audit-log 'update' (kind: 'print_render') → application/pdf
     inline. pos/sales/[id]/content.tsx printReceipt() rewired
     from window.print() (which would print the staff UI itself)
     to window.open('/api/print/sale/'+id, '_blank'). i18n keys
     at pos.print.* in both en.ts + es.ts. Commit pending.

 175. BUY-OUTRIGHT RECEIPT PDF — BILINGUAL EN+ES. Three new files.
     lib/pdf/BuyReceiptPDF.tsx (component): Letter, header with
     receipt number `BO-{LAST8(compliance_log.id)}`, customer
     block (name + DOB + address + phone + ID type + ID number),
     items table (description with optional S/N suffix / metal+
     karat / weight in grams / melt value at buy / payout), total
     payout (no balance — buy-outright is paid in full at intake
     by definition), mandatory hold-period notice (parameterized
     by days from settings.buy_hold_period_days, default 30 = FL),
     customer-acknowledgement block ("I am the lawful owner...
     received the total payout shown above in full satisfaction"),
     three signature lines (customer / staff / date), footer.
     lib/pdf/render-buy-receipt.tsx: sources from compliance_log
     (write-once buy snapshot — Rule 15), reads settings.buy_hold_
     period_days for the hold-period text, queries live
     inventory_items.hold_until and uses the LONGEST across the
     transaction's items (defensive against legal-hold extensions
     that override the tenant-default). app/api/print/buy/[id]/
     route.ts: path param is the anchor inventory_item_id (same
     convention as /buy/[id] page). Resolves item → tenant via
     admin (uniform with the receipt page itself) →
     requireRoleInTenant → render → audit-log → inline PDF.
     /buy/[id]/content.tsx Print button rewired from window.
     print() to the PDF endpoint, gated on the anchor item id
     being present. New i18n namespace buy.print.* in both en.ts
     + es.ts. Commit pending.

 176. NUMERIC `as any` CLEANUP — 19 CASTS REMOVED. Every `as any`
     cast in src/ turned out to be a missing-table-type or
     missing-column-type cast that's now resolved because
     database.ts has been regenerated since the casts were added
     (spot_prices, spot_price_overrides, ebay_listings, ebay_
     listing_events, stripe_payment_links, watch_models, and
     customers.auth_user_id are all in the generated types now).
     Removed across 13 files: lib/ebay/listings.ts (loadListing +
     persistListingPatch, 2), inventory/[id]/ebay-actions.ts
     (createEbayDraft + updateEbayDraft + publish + endListing +
     syncListing, 5), lib/ebay/client.ts (event insert, 1),
     (portal)/layout.tsx (portal customer lookup, 1), supabase/
     guards.ts (requireClientInTenant, 1), portal/stripe-payment-
     links.ts (table() helper, 1), portal/customer.ts (1),
     inventory/spot-prices/page.tsx (1), inventory/spot-prices/
     actions.tsx (1), spot-prices/refresh.ts (1), spot-prices/
     melt.ts (1), spot-prices/lookup.ts (getLatest + history, 2),
     api/portal/sign-in-bridge/route.ts (1). Where supabase-js's
     generic narrowing trips on NUMERIC's string-vs-number
     divergence at upsert/update time, the call site uses `as
     never` (single-call surface, NOT a project-wide cast).
     Where hand-rolled row types use `number` for NUMERIC fields,
     the row boundary uses `as unknown as T` to bridge the
     type-system gap with the runtime shape. Project-wide
     Database<'public'> override mapping NUMERIC → number remains
     on backlog as a proper fix that would replace the
     `as unknown as` pattern. Commit pending.

 177. VITEST SCAFFOLD + 36 PASSING TESTS ON lib/pawn/math.ts. New:
     vitest.config.ts (test environment 'node', `@/` alias
     mirroring next.config.ts, src/**/*.test.ts include glob,
     globals: false). package.json scripts: test (vitest run),
     test:watch (vitest), test:coverage (vitest run --coverage).
     devDeps: vitest@4.1.5 + @vitest/coverage-v8@4.1.5. Test
     file src/lib/pawn/math.test.ts covers 36 cases across r4
     (rounding contract + non-finite handling), toMoney (string
     / null / undefined paths), dailyRateFromMonthly,
     interestAccrued (zero-principal / zero-days / classic /
     partial-month / min_monthly_charge floor —
     REGRESSION-LOCKING the Session-12 Stripe-webhook
     under-billing incident — small-principal-with-min cases at
     30/15/1 days, large-principal-no-floor, zero-rate-with-min,
     min<=0 = no-floor), daysBetween (1 day, negative, zero
     same-day, month boundary, year boundary), addDaysIso
     (forward + backward), todayDateString (ISO format match),
     appliedPayments (string + number Supabase shapes, empty
     events), payoffBalance (zero-days = principal, classic
     30-day, prior interest-payment netting, partial principal,
     overpayment-of-interest clamped to 0, fully-paid principal
     stops billing further interest, min-charge passthrough,
     null-min = no-floor), splitPayment (zero-amount, ≤-owed,
     >-owed, zero-owed, negative-clamp). All 36 pass. Build
     floor, not ceiling — React rendering tests + Supabase mock
     surface deferred until a regression worth locking down
     arrives. Commit pending.

--- Session 14 (2026-04-29 night) — Wire production crons via vercel.json ---

 178. PRODUCTION CRONS WIRED — ROOT CAUSE OF SILENT FEED FAILURE.
     Diagnostic session triggered by the operator question "why isn't
     the live spot-price feed working?". Investigation: 5 cron routes
     under src/app/api/cron/* (refresh-spot-prices, maturity-
     reminders, layaway-reminders, repair-ready-reminders, ebay-sync)
     are each gated by `Authorization: Bearer ${CRON_SECRET}`. Two
     compounding causes for "no automatic runs": (1) vercel.json
     never existed in the repo, so Vercel has no schedule pointing
     at any /api/cron/* path — the routes had shipped over Sessions
     5/6/7/8/9 but never fired automatically since deploy; (2)
     CRON_SECRET still unset in Vercel project env (Phase 0 punch
     list item, open since Session 9). New vercel.json adds 6 cron
     entries: refresh-spot-prices x2 (`*/15 13-21 * * 1-5` + `0 *
     * * *`), maturity / layaway / repair-ready reminders staggered
     daily at 14:00 / 14:05 / 14:10 UTC, and ebay-sync at
     `0,30 13-21 * * 1-5` (STUBBED route; schedule lands preemptively
     so eBay wire-up needs no further config). Single-file commit
     c753654 pushed to main. CRON_SECRET still pending — schedules
     fire, route bodies return 401 until the operator sets it in
     Vercel project env. Confirmed Session 13 NUMERIC cleanup edit
     to refresh.ts did NOT cause the feed staleness — it improved
     inserted-count accuracy by chaining .select('id') after the
     upsert, but the insert path itself is unchanged. Plan caveat
     noted: Vercel Hobby tier rejects the sub-daily schedules and
     falls back to once-per-day; Pro+ accepts as written.

--- Session 15 (2026-04-29 night — late) — Spot-price source swap ---

 179. [CRITICAL] DEAD UPSTREAM — METALS.LIVE TLS ENDPOINT GONE.
     Operator reported "the pawn saas spot-price feature is not
     working" mid-session after a brief detour into the unrelated
     paracubasolutions.com gold widget (cleanly reverted). Live
     diagnostic via curl revealed api.metals.live returns
     SEC_E_ILLEGAL_MESSAGE on the TLS handshake — service is
     dead/decommissioned. Combined with Session 14's CRON_SECRET
     gap, the Pawn cron has been silently producing zero rows
     since whenever metals.live went down. Inventory melt values
     across the platform have been pinned to the migration 0013
     'seed' placeholder rows (gold=2400, silver=28, platinum=950,
     palladium=1100 USD/oz). Replaced with Yahoo Finance v8 chart
     endpoint — verified live for all four metals via curl + Node
     native fetch (gold $4575, silver $72.78, platinum $1915,
     palladium $1470 USD/oz at save time). Files touched:
     src/lib/spot-prices/sources/yahoo-finance.ts (NEW), deleted
     metals-live.ts, src/lib/spot-prices/refresh.ts (import +
     type rename + 5x source-label literals + comment),
     src/app/api/cron/refresh-spot-prices/route.ts (comment),
     src/app/(staff)/inventory/[id]/content.tsx (comment).
     Project tsc --noEmit clean. Commit ea68bdc pushed to main.
     Stooq (xauusd/xagusd/xptusd CSV, no auth) documented as a
     one-line swap if true LBMA spot ever matters.

--- Session 16 (2026-04-30 early-AM) — Stooq swap (Kitco-matching spot) ---

 180. [BUG] FUTURES-VS-SPOT GAP — YAHOO QUOTED $15 ABOVE KITCO.
     Operator compared the Session 15 Yahoo source against Kitco
     immediately after deploy: $4575 (Yahoo GC=F) vs $4559.70
     (Kitco). Web Claude diagnosed the gap correctly (COMEX gold
     futures trade in contango above LBMA spot — typical $5–15/oz
     premium) and recommended swapping `GC=F` → `XAUUSD=X`
     (Yahoo's spot ticker). Live test against Yahoo's v8/chart
     endpoint returned "No data found, symbol may be delisted"
     for XAUUSD=X. Yahoo has dropped that ticker from the chart
     endpoint as of 2026-04. Probed alternates: ^XAU is the
     Philadelphia gold/silver INDEX (not bullion), GLD is the
     SPDR Gold Trust ETF (NAV ~1/10th spot, expense-ratio drift),
     GOLD is the equity ticker for Barrick Gold — none return
     USD/oz spot. Pivoted to Stooq (the swap path I'd already
     documented as the alternative in yahoo-finance.ts). Stooq
     returns Close prices for xauusd / xagusd / xptusd / xpdusd
     within pennies of Kitco — verified live: gold $4562.105 vs
     Kitco $4559.70 = $2.40 gap (normal tick spread). Files:
     src/lib/spot-prices/sources/stooq.ts (NEW — CSV parser,
     polite UA, no auth), deleted yahoo-finance.ts, refresh.ts
     (import path swap; source label 'yahoo-finance' → 'stooq'
     across 5 places + header comment + error message),
     src/app/api/cron/refresh-spot-prices/route.ts (comment),
     src/app/(staff)/inventory/[id]/content.tsx (comment now
     enumerates 4 historical source values: 'stooq' / 'manual' /
     'seed' / older 'yahoo-finance' / 'metals.live'). tsc clean.
     Commit 344d822 pushed to main.

 183. RELEASE-BUY-HOLDS CRON ROUTE. Buy-outright items are
     inserted with `status='held'` + `hold_until = today +
     buy_hold_period_days` (FL = 30) and need to flip to
     'available' once the regulatory window passes. Until
     Session 17 the flip required a manual page action or
     a sale-time check. New file: src/app/api/cron/release-
     buy-holds/route.ts (selects items where status='held'
     AND hold_until IS NOT NULL AND hold_until <= today AND
     deleted_at IS NULL; bulk update via .in('id', ids); per-
     tenant audit_log row with item_ids + skus +
     hold_until_max for traceability; idempotent — second
     run finds zero matches). vercel.json gains a 6th cron
     entry: `15 14 * * *` daily (10:15 AM ET, well past
     midnight in any US TZ so all timezones have crossed
     into the new day). LOAD-BEARING DESIGN: the
     `hold_until IS NOT NULL` filter excludes layaway-held
     items (those use NULL because the layaway state
     machine flips them, not the date) — without the
     filter, the cron would incorrectly release reserved
     layaway items. Commit a1ef4ad pushed to main with
     items 181 + 182 + 183.

 184. NUMERIC OVERRIDE TASK CLOSED AS MOOT. Backlog item 16
     (Session 16 RESUME) called for a project-wide
     `Database<'public'>` override mapping NUMERIC → number
     to replace `as unknown as T` patterns. Verified the
     premise no longer holds: `src/types/database.ts`
     autogen already types every NUMERIC column as `number`
     (loans.principal: number, loans.interest_rate_monthly:
     number, layaways.balance_remaining: number,
     sales.subtotal: number, repair_ticket_items.unit_cost:
     number, appraisals.replacement_value: number, etc.). The
     Session 11 Progress.txt note about NUMERIC returning as
     `string` from db:types was true at that time but
     supabase-cli has since shipped a typegen update that
     maps NUMERIC → number directly. The 76 surviving `as
     unknown as` casts in src/ cover unrelated patterns: FK
     join relation shapes (~25 — supabase-js join inference
     is partial), JSONB column shapes (~10 — `Json` type is
     untyped), `as never` write bypasses (~5), partial-
     select bridging to full Row aliases (~20), and tables
     added since the last db:types run (~16). These are
     future cleanup targets but each requires a different
     pattern; lumping them under "NUMERIC override" was the
     wrong frame. Reported back; Eddy accepted the closure
     and asked for kanban DnD + tests instead.

 185. KANBAN DRAG-AND-DROP. /repair/board cards now drag
     between the 5 active-status columns. Adds @dnd-kit/core
     + @dnd-kit/sortable + @dnd-kit/utilities (touch +

     card, useDroppable on each column; client-side

     send_to_qa / mark_complete / parts_received /

     Phase 10 Path A backlog item 8 (Session 18 PENDING)
     opens with the data spine. patches/0026-tenant-
     public-landing.sql adds 4 columns to `tenants`:
     `public_slug CITEXT` (CITEXT for case-insensitive
     UNIQUE without a normalizing trigger), `public_
     landing_enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE`,
     `public_about TEXT`, `public_hours JSONB`. Slug
     shape enforced by CHECK constraint:
     `length(public_slug::text) BETWEEN 3 AND 40 AND
     public_slug ~ '^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$'` — blocks
     leading/trailing/consecutive hyphens at the DB
     level; reserved-name list (admin/api/www/portal/
     staff/etc.) lives in app code so platform-infra
     additions don't need a migration. UNIQUE partial
     index on `public_slug WHERE NOT NULL` allows
     multiple NULL slugs while enforcing uniqueness on
     claimed ones. New RLS policy
     `tenants_public_landing_select` grants anon +
     authenticated SELECT when `public_landing_enabled
     = TRUE AND public_slug IS NOT NULL AND is_active
     = TRUE` — first policy in the project that grants
     anon-role row visibility on a tenant-scoped table;
     column-level filtering is handled by the resolver's

     edge-safe parser (strips port, lowercases, rejects
     apex / multi-level / reserved / malformed) — and
     `fetchPublicTenant(slug)` — async via the anon SSR
     client so the RLS policy is the gate, narrows

 192. PROXY SUBDOMAIN REWRITE. proxy.ts gains a branch
     BEFORE the public-paths check that reads
     NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_DOMAIN, extracts the tenant
     subdomain via extractTenantSubdomain(host,
     baseDomain), and rewrites `acme.basedomain.com/X`
     → `/s/acme/X`. Same RSC handles both URL forms.
     Short-circuits cleanly when NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_DOMAIN
     is empty — the path form `/s/<slug>` works in
     local dev pre-DNS and on Vercel preview URLs
     without any DNS configuration. `/s` added to
     PUBLIC_PATHS so /s/<slug> bypasses auth. Path /
     subdomain branching avoids tenant-subdomain hosts
     hitting the staff dashboard at staff path, and
     vice versa: tenant subdomains serve the landing
     surface only (anything else under acme.basedomain
     .com 404s by design).

     columns + three RLS policies + partial index

     `public_catalog_enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT
     FALSE` — decoupled from public_landing_enabled
     so a tenant can publish a landing without
     exposing inventory. inventory_items gains
     `is_hidden_from_catalog BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT
     FALSE` — per-item escape hatch for consigned-
     under-negotiation, photos-pending-retake,
     back-room stock. Three SELECT-only RLS policies:
     `inventory_items_public_catalog_select`,
     `inventory_item_photos_public_catalog_select`,
     `inventory_item_stones_public_catalog_select`.
     All gated on three-AND tenant flags
     (public_landing_enabled + public_catalog_enabled
     + has_retail + is_active) plus per-row
     predicates (status='available' AND
     is_hidden_from_catalog = FALSE AND list_price
     IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL). Photo +
     stone policies join through inventory_items so
     the gate cascades. New partial index
     idx_inventory_public_catalog matches the RLS
     USING clause for cheap public reads regardless
     of total inventory size. NOTIFY pgrst at
     bottom; documented rollback (DROP POLICY x3 +

--- Session 21 (2026-05-05, mid-session checkpoint) — Phase 10 Path A slice 3/N: loyalty + referrals (DESIGN ONLY) ---

 205. LOYALTY/REFERRALS SPEC LOCKED. Brainstorming
     skill drove a 5-question design Q&A:
     (Q1) earning model → points wallet (vs store
     credit vs both); (Q2) earning events →
     retail subtotal + loan interest paid (vs
     retail-only / +repair / +layaway); (Q3)
     referral attribution → on referred customer's
     first qualifying transaction (vs on signup
     /  per-transaction kickback / no-referral);
     (Q4) POS redemption flow → cashier-driven
     points→discount on existing sales.discount
     (vs reward catalog / customer-generated code
     / auto-apply); (Q5a) gate+config in
     `settings` table (vs split with tenants);
     (Q5b) forward-only earning (no backfill);
     (Q5c) manual staff adjustments allowed
     (manager+, audit-logged, required reason).
     Architecture choice locked: materialized
     customers.loyalty_points_balance + AFTER
     INSERT trigger on loyalty_events
     (SECURITY DEFINER + locked search_path).
     Idempotency: partial UNIQUE index on
     (customer_id, source_kind, source_id, kind)
     WHERE kind IN auto-credit set so manual +
     redeem events can repeat freely. Referral
     codes: 6-char A-Z + 2-9 (no I/O/0/1), per-
     tenant unique, lazy-generated via
     ensureReferralCode. Spec written to
     docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-04-loyalty-
     referrals-design.md (extensive — 8 sections
     covering DB + integration hooks + staff UX +
     portal UX + i18n + testing + out-of-scope
     deferrals + 15-step migration sequencing).
     Self-review fixed two ambiguities inline.
     Awaiting Eddy's review before invoking the
     writing-plans skill. NO CODE CHANGES YET.

--- Session 22 (2026-05-05, end-of-session) — Phase 10 Path A slice 3/N: loyalty + referrals SHIPPED ---

 206. SPEC §9 AMENDMENTS LOCKED + WRITING-PLANS
     GENERATED. Before touching code, locked five
     design amendments inline in the spec at
     docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-04-loyalty-
     referrals-design.md §9.1-§9.13: (1) append-only
     DB enforcement on loyalty_events via BEFORE
     UPDATE OR DELETE trigger; (2) same-tenant
     referral guard via BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF
     referred_by_customer_id trigger on customers;
     (3) CHECK constraints on all four rate columns

     redemption_rate to prevent divide-by-zero); (4)
     earn-clawback on void (full) and partial return

     earn_loan_interest, earn_referral_bonus,
     redeem_pos, redeem_undo, earn_clawback,
     adjust_manual); 2 ordinary indexes
     (idx_loyalty_events_customer_created (customer_id,
     created_at DESC) + idx_loyalty_events_tenant); 1
     UNIQUE PARTIAL idempotency index on
     (customer_id, source_kind, source_id, kind)
     WHERE kind IN auto-credit-set (4 kinds:
     earn_sale, earn_loan_interest,
     earn_referral_bonus, earn_clawback); 1 UNIQUE
     PARTIAL referral_code index per-tenant; 3
     trigger functions (apply-balance with SECURITY
     DEFINER + locked search_path; block-mutation
     without SECURITY DEFINER; same-tenant-referral
     with SECURITY DEFINER + locked search_path); 3
     RLS policies on loyalty_events (staff SELECT,
     staff INSERT with defense-in-depth performed_by
     self-check, portal SELECT via auth.uid() =
     customers.auth_user_id). NOTIFY pgrst at end.
     Full reverse-order rollback documented.

 208. PURE-LOGIC + TDD: lib/loyalty/math.ts. Wrote
     test file FIRST (red), then implementation
     (green). 7 exports: computeRetailEarn,
     computeLoanInterestEarn,
     computeRedemptionDiscount with cap-then-shrink-
     pointsConsumed semantics, generateReferralCode
     (32-char alphabet ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789
     no I/O/0/1, 6 chars), canApplyAdjustment,
     isValidReferralCode, clampClawback. 31 vitest
     tests across 7 describe blocks. Code-review
     surfaced two real correctness gaps:
     computeRedemptionDiscount missed Number.isFinite
     guards (NaN slipped past <= 0 check, could
     silently corrupt balance) and pointsConsumed in
     un-capped branch wasn't floored (could violate
     INTEGER column with stale fractional input).
     Both fixed with TDD red-then-green discipline.
     Test count climbed 342 → 373.

 209. SINGLE-WRITER lib/loyalty/events.ts (10 helpers,
     1 internal insertEvent). EVERY loyalty_events
     INSERT in the codebase routes through this
     module per spec §9.8. Internal insertEvent
     returns discriminated InsertResult
     {ok:true,id} | {ok:'collision'} | {ok:false,error}
     so callers distinguish success from idempotency
     collision (23505 unique-violation) from real
     error. Code-review-driven fix:
     maybeCreditReferral flips referral_credited
     ONLY on success or collision (NOT on real
     failure — preserves retry path; pre-fix would
     silently corrupt referral state on transient
     DB errors). ensureReferralCode loop tightened
     to distinguish UNIQUE collision (continue),
     0-rows-affected (re-read for concurrent set),
     non-collision error (throw with real message).
     applyReferredByCode UPDATE gated with
     `.is('referred_by_customer_id', null)` for
     idempotency under races. recordEarnClawback
     internally clamps via clampClawback against
     current balance — actions never pre-clamp, so
     void/return cannot fail on insufficient points.

 210. EARN/REFERRAL/CLAWBACK HOOKS across 5 server
     actions, all routing through helpers (no inline
     INSERT INTO loyalty_events anywhere outside
     events.ts):
     - completeSaleAction (pos/sales/[id]):
       recordEarnSale on sale.subtotal (NOT total —
       no points on tax) + maybeCreditReferral
     - recordPaymentAction (pawn/[id]): captures
       loan_event id, recordEarnLoanInterest keyed
       on loan_event.id when interest_paid > 0,
       maybeCreditReferral
     - redeemLoanAction (pawn/[id]): same pattern
       for the payoff loan_event, gated on
       split.interest_paid > 0
     - voidSaleAction (pos/sales/[id]): SUMs
       earn_sale rows for the sale → single
       recordEarnClawback (sourceKind='sale',
       reason='sale_voided'); iterates redeem_pos
       rows → recordRedeemUndo for each (positive
       delta = abs(original points_delta))
     - createReturnAction (pos/returns/new):
       recordEarnClawback proportional to returned
       subtotal (sourceKind='return',
       sourceId=ret.id, reason='sale_returned')

 211. /settings/loyalty page+content+actions (owner +
     chain_admin only). Master enable + 4 numeric
     rate inputs (earn_rate_retail, earn_rate_loan_
     interest, redemption_rate, referral_bonus).
     Zod schema mirrors DB CHECKs: gt(0) on
     redemption_rate, int().min(0) on referral_bonus,
     min(0).max(1000) on earn rates. Action calls

 213. POSREDEMPTIONBLOCK + redeemPointsOnSaleAction
     + undoRedemptionAction. Block rendered below
     totals on /pos/sales/[id], gated on
     loyalty.enabled && sale.customer_id; returns
     null when not applicable (sale not open AND
     no balance + no prior redemptions on this
     sale). "Redeem points" form with live preview;
     existing redemptions render with Undo. Action
     uses real sales.discount_amount + tax_amount
     + total columns (NOT sale.discount which
     doesn't exist). Multi-redemption per sale is
     supported because redeem_pos kind is
     intentionally OUTSIDE the idempotency partial-
     unique index. UNDO writes a redeem_undo
     compensating event (positive delta) — never
     deletes; matches the project's append-only
     posture.

 223. PLACES API CLIENT + CACHE LAYER
     (lib/google-reviews/{client,cache}.ts). client.ts
     ('server-only') wraps Google Places Details API
     with one exported function fetchPlaceDetails;
     never throws — returns either PlaceDetails or
     {error:string}; cache: 'no-store' (DB cache is
     the cache layer); language='en' for cache
     stability. cache.ts is the most subtle file
     in the slice — three exports + two internals:
     getCachedReviews (4-branch logic per spec
     §2.2: !placeId, fresh hit, stale SWR via
     after(), no-row-or-mismatch awaits refresh);
     refreshReviews (resolves API key per-tenant-
     first then env fallback, calls
     fetchPlaceDetails, UPSERTs success row OR
     calls writeError preserving previous good
     payload); loadPublicReviews (refactored to
     single-arg tenantId — reads
     settings.google_reviews_min_star_floor itself
     in parallel with getCachedReviews via
     Promise.all); loadCachedRow + writeError
     placeholder-row pattern. Subagent code-quality
     review caught two real Important issues:
     (a) writeError INSERT branch silently swallowed
         non-PK errors — fixed to capture insertErr
         and console.error non-23505 codes
         (PK collision is documented-benign);
     (b) `void refreshReviews(...)` would not
         complete in serverless — replaced with
         `after(() => refreshReviews(...))` from
         'next/server' which registers with the
         runtime's waitUntil. Both fixed inline
         + plan + Progress.txt updated to reflect.

 232. CRON WARMER FOR GOOGLE REVIEWS. Public-path
     after() refresh covers tenants with traffic;
     this cron covers no-traffic tenants whose 24h
     TTL otherwise expires before the next visitor.
     New: src/app/api/cron/refresh-google-reviews/
     route.ts. Schedule '0 */6 * * *'. Threshold
     18h (75% of TTL) — refreshes anything older
     than that. Sequential dispatch to avoid
     Places API bursts. Audit-logs one row per
     tenant with action='google_reviews_refresh'.
     Gated on CRON_SECRET like the other 6 cron
     routes (x-vercel-cron is NOT a security
     check). Cost-bounded: each tenant gets at
     most 1 Places API call per ~18-24h.

 233. PER-TENANT GOOGLE PLACES API QUOTA
     GUARDRAILS. Concrete risk: if a tenant's API
     key is bad or place_id stale, every public
     visitor triggers a Places API call (because
     row stays stale-with-error). Cap protects
     platform free tier and detects misconfigured
     cache loops. Patch 0030 adds tenant_google_
     reviews.quota_window_start + quota_calls_used,
     settings.google_reviews_daily_quota
     (per-tenant override; NULL = platform default
     50). Atomic SECURITY DEFINER RPC consume_
     google_reviews_quota with FOR UPDATE row lock,
     rolling 24h window reset. lib/google-reviews/
     cache.ts:refreshReviews charges quota before
     fetchPlaceDetails; degrades open if RPC errors
     (so a quota service hiccup doesn't break
     widget for everyone).

 234. PER-REVIEW HIDE LIST. Patch 0031 adds
     settings.google_reviews_hidden_review_times
     BIGINT[] keyed on Google review.time (Unix
     seconds — closest thing to stable ID).

     paginated to clear the 1000-row default),
     snapshotRecipients (lazy-generates
     email_unsubscribe_token UUID for any
     recipient missing one; UPSERT with ON
     CONFLICT dedupe), dispatchCampaign (drains
     queued recipients in batches of 25; re-
     checks customer.marketing_opt_in at send
     time = compliance; renders with
     {{customer_first_name}} + {{unsubscribe_
     url}} merge fields via existing render-
     EmailTemplate; calls existing sendEmail()
     with kind='email_campaign'),
     recomputeAggregates, pickNextCampaign
     (resume sending first, else earliest
     scheduled), buildUnsubscribeUrl.

 237. EMAIL CAMPAIGNS — CRON ROUTE. New
     src/app/api/cron/send-email-campaigns/
     route.ts. Schedule '*/2 * * * *'. One
     campaign per tick — picks pickNext-
     Campaign(), snapshots if 'scheduled',
     dispatches, flips to 'sent' when queue
     drains, recomputes aggregates. If timeout
     mid-flight, next tick resumes from
     'sending' state. Audit-logs every run with
     action='email_campaign_dispatch' + the
     phase (sent / sent_empty / dispatch_
     partial). Audit-action union extended
     with email_campaign_create/update/
     schedule/cancel/dispatch/unsubscribe/
     bounce/complaint.

 238. PUBLIC UNSUBSCRIBE PAGE. New src/app/
     (public)/unsubscribe/{page,content,actions}.
     tsx. proxy.ts PUBLIC_PATHS extended with
     /unsubscribe. Token UUID validated via
     regex; bad/expired token resolves to
     identical "invalid" view (no info
     disclosure). Confirm form posts via
     unsubscribeAction → flips marketing_opt_in
     to false → redirects to /unsubscribe?ok=1
     so success state survives refresh and the
     token doesn't leak in browser history.
     Bilingual EN+ES copy inline in content.tsx
     with useI18n().lang lookup (one-off public
     surface — not worth a full i18n catalog
     section). Audit-logs with action=
     'email_campaign_unsubscribe',
     via='public_unsubscribe_link'.

 239. RESEND BOUNCE/COMPLAINT WEBHOOK. New
     src/app/api/webhooks/resend/route.ts.
     Svix signature verification inline (no
     svix dependency — Node crypto + timing-
     safe compare; ~30 LOC). Strips whsec_
     prefix and base64-decodes the secret;
     loops over space-separated v1,<sig>
     entries in the svix-signature header
     (Svix rotates so multiple may be active).
     Handles email.delivered (mirror to
     message_log delivered_at + recipient row);
     email.bounced (sets bounced_at +
     bounce_reason; if not soft_bounce/
     transient, flips marketing_opt_in to
     false on customer + audits); email.
     complained (always flips marketing_opt_
     in; complainants tank platform sender
     rep). Recomputes campaign aggregates
     after each event. Acknowledges unknown
     event types so Resend doesn't retry.
     Falls back to message_log update for
     transactional emails not tied to a
     campaign recipient row.

 240. SUPABASE VAULT — MIGRATION 0033. Patch
     0033 creates public.tenant_secrets (id,
     tenant_id FK tenants, kind TEXT, vault_
     secret_id UUID, created_at, updated_at,
     UNIQUE(tenant_id, kind)). RLS enabled
     with NO POLICIES — only service_role can
     touch the table (bypasses RLS). RPCs
     set_tenant_secret(tenant_id, kind, value)
     and get_tenant_secret(tenant_id, kind),
     both SECURITY DEFINER + REVOKE FROM
     PUBLIC + GRANT EXECUTE TO service_role.
     set_tenant_secret rotates in place via
     vault.update_secret when a row exists;
     creates fresh via vault.create_secret
     otherwise; treats empty value as clear
     (DELETE both vault.secrets row and
     registry entry). get_tenant_secret looks
     up vault_secret_id then SELECTs from
     vault.decrypted_secrets. Backfill DO
     block iterates 8 secret kinds across
     settings + tenant_billing_settings +
     tenant_ebay_credentials, copying any
     existing plaintext into vault — idem-
     potent (re-running is a no-op). Plain-
     text columns intentionally NOT dropped
     in this migration; deferred to migration
     0034 once read+write paths cut over.

 241. VAULT TS HELPER. New src/lib/secrets/
     vault.ts: typed SecretKind union (8
     kinds), getTenantSecret, setTenantSecret,
     resolveSecret (vault-first / plaintext-
     fallback during dual-state). All call
     the new RPCs via .rpc() through the admin
     client.

 242. VAULT CUTOVER — COMMS (resend_api_key +
     twilio_auth_token). lib/email/send.ts
     resolveResendCreds wraps the API key
     read in resolveSecret('resend_api_key',
     plaintext-fallback). lib/twilio/sms.ts

     auth_token. settings/communications/

 243. VAULT CUTOVER — GOOGLE_PLACES_API_KEY.

 244. VAULT CUTOVER — EBAY_ACCESS_TOKEN +
     EBAY_REFRESH_TOKEN. lib/ebay/auth.ts:
     persistTokens dual-writes both tokens

     update. markDisconnected clears both

     registry entry). resolveCredentials

     Stripe vault cutover is a no-op today —

     billing_settings but no read or write
     paths use them yet (Connect onboarding
     not built; current Stripe wiring is
     platform-level env vars). Migration 0033

     free; helper is ready when Connect lands.

--- Session 27 (2026-05-06, ~11:00) — Pawn ticket backpage policy text (per-tenant override + FL Ch. 539 default) ---

 256. PATCHES/0035-PAWN-TICKET-BACKPAGE.SQL —
     SINGLE-COLUMN ADDITIVE MIGRATION. ALTER
     TABLE public.settings ADD COLUMN IF NOT
     EXISTS pawn_ticket_backpage TEXT NULL with
     a COMMENT documenting the default-fallback
     contract ("NULL = use the FL Ch. 539
     default shipped with the renderer; English
     only — the ticket is a legal document").
     Idempotent — IF NOT EXISTS makes re-run
     safe. Ends with NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload
     schema' so PostgREST can see the new
     column without a Supabase API restart.
     Operator applied; db:types regen post-
     0035 picked up the new column on Row +
     Insert + Update of the settings table.

 257. PAWN-TICKET-BACKPAGE-DEFAULT.TS — FL CH.
     539 STANDARD TEXT AS A TS CONSTANT. New
     src/lib/pdf/pawn-ticket-backpage-default.ts
     exports PAWN_TICKET_BACKPAGE_DEFAULT
     containing the operator-pasted FL Ch. 539
     standard pawn ticket reverse text:
     pledged-goods deposit clause, anti-stolen-
     goods warranty, 30-day automatic-
     forfeiture rule, 25%-per-30-days service
     charge breakdown (with 2% interest /
     remainder = storage and service fees
     allocation), redemption-within-first-30
     vs 31-60-day rate rules, extension daily-
     rate computation (1/30 of original),
     redemption-ID requirements (firearms-
     only-by-original-pledgor, attorney-in-
     fact + authorized-representative rules
     with notarized authorization for the
     latter), false-ID felony language with
     specific F.S. cites (s.775.082/083/084),
     lost-ticket procedure (certified mail /
     in-person), shop liability cap on
     lost/damaged goods (replacement-with-
     like-kind defense), attorney's-fee shift
     to losing party, mail-redemption terms,
     no-oral-modification clause, the asterisk
     bullet list of "PROPER ID REQUIRED" /
     "FIREARMS ONLY BY ORIGINAL PLEDGOR" / etc.,
     a separator, the LOST PAWN TICKET
     STATEMENT form ($2.00 fee + date +
     lost/destroyed/stolen circle + Pledgor +
     Pledgor's I.D. + Employee/PS), another
     separator, and the I HEREBY ACKNOWLEDGE
     RECEIPT block with redeemer signature line +
     "REDEEMER'S IDENTIFICATION IF OTHER THAN
     ORIGINAL PLEDGOR" sub-form (Name / Address
     / ID Number and Type) + thumbprint label.
     Sections separated by `--` lines, paragraphs
     by blank lines, form-field lines use long
     underscore runs (5+ underscores). The
     constant ships in code so improvements to
     the default reach every uncustomized
     tenant on next print without a backfill
     migration.

 258. PAWNTICKETPDF.TSX — SECOND <Page> FOR
     REVERSE-SIDE LEGAL BLOCK. Added
     backpage_text: string field to PawnTicketData;
     new backpage style block (smaller font 7.5pt,
     tighter line-height 1.35, mono variant for
     form-field lines, marginVertical=6 for the
     `--` rules); new parseBackpageBlocks(text)
     helper at module scope that:
       1. Normalizes \r\n → \n
       2. Splits on /\n{2,}/ for paragraph
          boundaries
       3. Tags `^--+$` paragraphs as `kind:
          'rule'` (renders as a horizontal rule)
       4. Tags paragraphs containing any line
          matching /_{3,}/ as `mono: true`
          (renders in JetBrains Mono so 5+
          underscores form continuous handwriting
          rules — Inter would render gappy due
          to kerning)
       5. Returns a typed BackpageBlock[]
     New <Page size="LETTER"> after the front
     page renders the parsed blocks: header
     "Terms and Conditions — Reverse Side"
     (uppercase tracking, ink color), then for
     each block either a divider <View> with
     borderBottom or a paragraph <View> wrapping
     each line as its own <Text> (preserves
     line breaks within paragraphs without
     react-pdf collapsing them). Reuses the
     existing footer template (page n of total
     bilingual) inside the backpage Page so
     pagination spans both pages naturally.

--- Session 30 (2026-05-06, ~14:30) — Layout shell follow-up: sidebar full-height ---

 271. SIDEBAR FULL-HEIGHT LAYOUT FIX. Operator
     reported after Session 29's commit landed:
     "the menu doesn't go all the way to the top,
     the header is in the way." Root cause:
     src/app/(staff)/layout.tsx wrapped the
     entire shell in `<div className="flex
     min-h-screen flex-col">` — header was the
     FIRST child (full width across the top),
     sidebar/main row was the SECOND child.
     That stacked the navy sidebar UNDER a
     full-width white header, leaving a 64px
     white strip above the navy column. The
     Luna Azul reference puts the sidebar
     adjacent to the topbar, not below it.
     Fix: removed `flex-col` from the outer
     wrapper, moved Sidebar to be the first
     child, wrapped the header + main in a
     new `<div className="flex flex-1 flex-col">`
     right column. Net structure now:
     `<flex min-h-screen> [Sidebar] [flex-col
     {header h-16, main}]`. Result: navy column
     runs y=0 → bottom-of-viewport; topbar
     only spans the right portion (next to the
     sidebar, not above it). No content / styling
     /component changes — pure parent-element
     restructure. tsc --noEmit exit 0. Single-
     file diff (src/app/(staff)/layout.tsx).

     commit 7c510f1). src/app/(staff)/customers/
     dashboard/[id]/page.tsx threw 2 eslint
     react-hooks/purity errors: "Cannot call impure
     function during render" on inline Date.now() at
     :279 (daysSinceLastSale) and :392 (sixtyDaysAgo).
     ROOT CAUSE: the rule bans Date.now() in render;
     new Date() (no-arg) is NOT flagged. FIX: hoisted
     one "const now = new Date()" + "const nowMs =
     now.getTime()" to the top of the derived-metrics

     removed the duplicate "const now = new Date()"
     that previously sat at the 12-month-trends block.

       unit-tests under vitest.

       existing STOP any-case, empty untouched).

     - src/lib/twilio/whatsapp.ts: reduced to a creds

 309. MIGRATION 0037 BACKFILL FIX (commit
     56d53ea). Operator hit error:
     `column "deleted_at" does not exist` when
     applying 0037. Root cause: tenants table
     uses `is_active BOOLEAN` for soft-suspend,
     not the `deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ` pattern
     other tables use. Fixed the backfill DO
     block to loop over ALL tenant rows (the
     ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING in the seed
     function makes per-row work idempotent).
     The whole migration is idempotent so
     re-running is safe.

     wizard step 1 — pick top-level → sub-tile
     row reveals → pick sub → advance to step 2.
     Top-levels with no subs (General) advance
     directly.
     - patches/0038-pawn-intake-subcategories.sql:
       ADD COLUMN parent_id UUID self-FK with
       ON DELETE CASCADE. DROP UNIQUE(tenant_id,
       slug), CREATE UNIQUE INDEX scoped by
       COALESCE(parent_id, '00000000-...'::
       UUID) so a sub of one top can share a
       slug with a sub of another top.
       seed_pawn_intake_subcategories() seeds 18
       defaults (Jewelry: Rings/Chains/Bracelets/
       Earrings/Pendants/Watches; Electronics:
       Phones/Laptops/Tablets/Consoles/Audio/TVs;
       Firearms: Handguns/Rifles/Shotguns;
       Tools: Power tools/Hand tools/Yard tools;
       General: none — catch-all). tenant
       AFTER INSERT trigger updated to call
       both seeders. Backfill loops existing
       tenants.
     - CategoryPicker rewritten with internal
       drilledTop state. CategoryBanner shows
       "Jewelry → Ring [Change]".
     - /pawn/new/page.tsx fetches all rows,
       groups subs under parents in JS, filters
       firearms-flagged tiles when has_firearms
       =false (BOTH top-levels AND sub-rows
       checked).
     - /pawn/new/form.tsx tracks both
       categorySlug + subcategorySlug. Hidden
       fields pawn_category + pawn_subcategory
       ride on submit. categoryStepDone gate
       requires both (or top with no subs) to
       reveal the rest of the form.
     - /settings/pawn-categories list view
       nests subs under their parent with
       border-l indent + "+ Add sub" pill button
       per top-level row. Modal gains a Parent
       (None = top-level) dropdown that pre-fills
       to the row being edited or the parent
       being added under.
     - saveCategoryAction accepts parent_id
       (UUID or null/'none' string variants).

 311. CLAUDE-MD-WORTHY GOTCHA: TENANTS USES
     is_active NOT deleted_at. Discovered via
     item 309 above. Future migration writers
     who loop over tenants for backfill must
     filter by is_active OR loop all rows
     (depending on whether suspended tenants

 288. STT PROVIDER DECISION — KEEP WHISPER, GET
     OPENAI KEY. Operator revealed that the value
     pasted into OPENAI_API_KEY in Vercel is
     actually an Anthropic key — operator doesn't
     have an OpenAI account. Investigated whether
     Claude could replace Whisper: grep'd
     @anthropic-ai/sdk/resources for audio
     content-block types, confirmed Anthropic
     Messages API supports text + image +
     document content blocks but NOT audio (as of
     the installed SDK version). So a transcription
     provider must come from outside Anthropic.
     Surfaced two options via AskUserQuestion:
     (A) browser Web Speech API — free, no key,
     Chrome/Edge-only client-side transcription;
     (B) OpenAI Whisper — keep current
     architecture, $5 minimum signup, better
     accuracy, broader browser support.
     Operator chose (B). No code changes —
     operator will sign up at platform.openai.com,
     load $5, copy the sk-proj-... key, replace
     the current OPENAI_API_KEY value in Vercel,
     and redeploy. Decision rationale documented
     in the new RESUME block: pawn shop runs on
     Windows AND iPads, so cross-browser support
     matters more than the $5 floor.

 289. CLAUDE-DOES-NOT-TRANSCRIBE-AUDIO RULE
     LOCKED. Investigation in item 288 confirmed
     that as of @anthropic-ai/sdk currently in
     package.json, audio is not a supported
     content-block type on the Messages API.
     Documented in the Session 34 RESUME's KEY
     DECISIONS so future sessions don't repeat
     the investigation. If/when Anthropic ships
     audio support, the voice route can be
     simplified to a single Anthropic call;
     until then Whisper (or an equivalent
     non-Anthropic STT) stays.

--- Archived 2026-11-21 ---

--- Session 52 (2026-09-21) — Security: profiles privesc CLOSED + real browser smoke harness ---

 380. [CRITICAL] profiles PRIVILEGE ESCALATION — CONFIRMED LIVE
     VULNERABLE, THEN CLOSED (patches/0056). The scaffold hole flagged
     2026-07-03 and deferred four sessions was real here. Read-only
     audit against the live DB found `anon` AND `authenticated` holding
     table-level UPDATE on EVERY profiles column — role and tenant_id
     included — while policy profiles_update_own was
     FOR UPDATE USING (id = auth.uid()) with NO WITH CHECK, so Postgres
     reuses USING for the check and it pins row ownership only.
     ROOT CAUSE: RLS was being asked to do column authorization, which
     it cannot do. A row policy says WHICH ROWS you may touch, never
     WHICH COLUMNS. The grant layer is the column control, and Supabase's
     default grants had handed out everything.
     Exploit: PATCH /rest/v1/profiles?id=eq.<self> {"role":"superadmin"}.
     Payoff verified as total — my_is_superadmin() short-circuits every
     tenant policy, AND billing_invoices + tenant_subscriptions gate
     directly on profiles.role = 'superadmin'.

 381. THE FIX IS TWO LAYERS, NOT ONE (patches/0056).
     (a) GRANT layer — the actual control. REVOKE INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/
         TRUNCATE/REFERENCES on profiles FROM anon, authenticated; then
         GRANT UPDATE (full_name, avatar_url, language) TO authenticated.
         Column list chosen for THIS app, not copied from Tocoloro.
         `email` deliberately excluded: it mirrors auth.users.email and
         renders as identity in /audit and /team, so self-editing it lets
         one staffer impersonate another in the audit trail.
         created_at/updated_at excluded — a trigger writing a column does
         not require the CALLER to hold UPDATE on it.
     (b) Guard trigger trg_profiles_guard_privilege_columns — refuses any
         role/tenant_id change made under an end-user JWT. Exists because
         a lone REVOKE is one careless GRANT ALL (or one scaffold re-run)
         away from being undone. Escape hatch is a TRANSACTION-LOCAL GUC
         (app.profile_privilege_write) set only by
         claim_tenant_with_license_key, which 0056 re-creates for that
         purpose — that RPC is SECURITY DEFINER but is CALLED by the
         onboarding user, so their JWT is still on the connection.
     NO RLS POLICY WAS TOUCHED (CLAUDE.md rule 1).

 382. COMPANION SCAFFOLD HOLE — NOT APPLICABLE HERE. The
     schema_migrations-without-RLS hole does not exist in this project:
     there is no schema_migrations table (migrations run through
     scripts/apply-migration.mjs), and the audit confirmed every table in
     `public` already has RLS enabled. Also swept every other
     role-bearing table for the same ownership-pinned-UPDATE pattern —
     profiles_update_own was the ONLY one. user_tenants has no UPDATE
     policy at all, so the per-tenant role table was never exposed.

 383. VERIFICATION (live DB, every test forced to roll back via a
     sentinel RAISE inside its own subtransaction).
       authenticated SET role=superadmin       -> BLOCKED 42501
       authenticated SET tenant_id             -> BLOCKED 42501
       grant layer bypassed (owner) + user JWT -> BLOCKED by trigger
       service_role / admin client writes role -> still allowed
       license-key onboarding escape hatch     -> still allowed
       hatch off again in same txn             -> BLOCKED (no leak)
       language / full_name / avatar_url       -> still self-editable
     NOTE FOR FUTURE PROBES: the first trigger test produced a FALSE
     NEGATIVE ("trigger did not fire"). This DB has exactly one profile
     and it is already role='superadmin', so the test set superadmin ->
     superadmin; the trigger correctly saw no privileged change. A
     privilege-column test must assert the value actually CHANGES.

 384. REAL BROWSER SMOKE HARNESS (scripts/smoke-browser.mjs,
     `npm run smoke:browser`). Checked Luna Azul as Eddy suggested — its
     Playwright is playwright-core driving a REMOTE Browserbase session
     for the DViajero scraping agent (src/lib/dviajero/agent.ts), not an
     e2e harness, and there are no local browsers. So Pawn got its own:
     playwright-core as a devDependency, driving installed Chrome via
     executablePath (no browser download, no revision-matching against
     the ms-playwright cache). Signs in FOR REAL — admin generateLink ->
     verifyOtp -> session cookies produced by driving @supabase/ssr
     itself rather than hand-rolling its cookie format — then drives 30
     staff routes reporting per route: HTTP status, unexpected redirects,
     uncaught exceptions, console errors, failed subresource requests and
     untranslated i18n keys found in RENDERED TEXT. --gates flips the two
     feature-4 module flags on for the run and restores them in a finally
     block. --only / --repeat / --verbose for chasing flaky failures.
     Screenshots to .smoke/ (gitignored). Exit 1 on any failure.

 385. [BUG] APP-WIDE HYDRATION MISMATCH, FOUND BY THE NEW HARNESS
     (src/components/version-footer.tsx). VersionFooter called
     relativeTime(buildTime) DURING RENDER, and relativeTime reads
     Date.now(). Server emitted "12s ago", client hydrated "13s ago"
     whenever the two renders straddled a second boundary. React's diff
     named it exactly: +13s ago / -12s ago under <VersionFooter>.
     ROOT CAUSE: a time-derived value computed in render on both sides of
     the SSR boundary. Because the footer sits in the ROOT LAYOUT, the
     hydration error fired on EVERY page and React threw away the server
     HTML for that subtree to re-render it client-side.
     Worst in dev (buildTime is seconds old, so the Ns granularity makes
     a mismatch near-certain) but live too — right after a deploy and at
     any unit boundary (59s->60s, 59m->60m).
     FIX: buildAge starts as '' and is filled in from a useEffect after
     mount, so first paint agrees on both sides; a 30s interval also
     keeps it fresh instead of frozen at page-load time.

 386. A WARM DEV SERVER HIDES HYDRATION BUGS — the methodology lesson of
     this session. The harness's FIRST run reported 30/30 clean and that
     was a FALSE NEGATIVE: it had hit an already-compiled dev server
     (an orphan — TaskStop killed the npm wrapper but left `next dev`
     listening on :3060). The SAME code failed 30/30 against a cold one,
     because a warm server renders fast enough that both sides usually
     produce the same string. A clean smoke only counts from cold: stop
     the server BY PID, delete .next, restart, then smoke. Verified the
     fix that exact way -> 30/30 clean, tsc + lint + 497 tests green.

--- Session 51 (2026-09-19) — PawnSmarts-parity feature 4: store credit + consignment ---

 368. STORE CREDIT (patches/0052 + 0053). 0052 is enum-only —
     payment_method += 'store_credit' — because the Management API sends
     a patch as ONE transaction and Postgres refuses to use a new enum
     value in the transaction that adds it (same split as 0049/0050).
     0053 adds customers.store_credit_balance NUMERIC(18,4) maintained
     by an AFTER INSERT trigger on the new append-only
     store_credit_events ledger, with CHECK (>= 0) as the safety net and
     a per-kind sign CHECK so an app-side sign slip cannot corrupt a
     balance. Seven kinds: issue_return / issue_buy / issue_manual /
     redeem_pos / redeem_undo / clawback / adjust_manual. A partial
     unique index gives idempotency on the issue + undo kinds (a
     double-submitted return cannot credit twice; one redemption cannot
     be undone twice) while leaving redeem_pos and manual adjustments
     repeatable on purpose. settings.store_credit_enabled (off by
     default) + settings.store_credit_expiry_days (informational ONLY —
     nothing expires credit, it is a liability and several states treat
     unclaimed balances as unclaimed property).

 369. THE REDEEM RPC IS THE WHOLE POINT (0053). A store-credit tender is
     three writes that must be atomic: the ledger debit, the
     sale_payments row, and the sales.paid_total roll-up. Doing them as
     three PostgREST calls from a server action leaves three ways to
     half-apply a tender — credit taken with no payment recorded, or a
     payment recorded against credit nobody was charged for. So
     store_credit_redeem_on_sale(tenant, sale, amount, by) does all
     three under FOR UPDATE locks on the sale and the customer, and
     'store_credit' is deliberately EXCLUDED from paymentMethodSchema
     so the generic add-payment path cannot reach it. Companions:
     store_credit_undo_sale_redemption() (open sale — soft-deletes the
     payment, backs out paid_total, writes the compensating row) and
     store_credit_restore_for_sale() (void path — leaves the payment
     rows as history, exactly like the card-refund path, and puts the
     credit back). All three are service_role-only; the guards run
     first (Rule 10). Errors are raised as stable tokens
     (store_credit_insufficient_balance etc.) so the UI translates them
     instead of showing Postgres text.

 370. STORE-CREDIT SURFACES. POS: a tender block on the sale detail
     (balance, "use max" capped by BOTH the balance and what the sale
     still owes, per-redemption undo). Customer detail: balance +
     5-row ledger + an owner/manager adjust modal where direction is a
     CHOICE and a reason is mandatory (a clerk typing "-50" for "50"
     must not be able to hand out money). Returns: 'store_credit' as a
     refund method, offered only with an identified customer and the
     module on, checked BEFORE the return row is written. Buy-outright:
     'store_credit' as a payout method — issued AFTER compliance_log so
     a purchase that never reached the police-report ledger cannot
     leave credit behind; if the credit write fails the purchase STANDS
     (compliance_log is write-once by design, Rule 15) and the error
     tells staff to issue it by hand. Receipt PDF prints "Store credit /
     Crédito de tienda". The daily register correctly ignores
     store-credit payments — they are neither drawer cash nor a card
     batch.

 371. CONSIGNMENT (patches/0054). consignors are ALWAYS backed by a
     customers row (never a second copy of someone's identity), carry
     the DEFAULT commission + payout method, and get a per-tenant
     'CN-000001'. inventory_items gains consignor_id +
     consignment_commission_pct (FROZEN at intake — changing the
     consignor default never re-prices goods already on the floor) +
     consignment_min_price + consignment_expires_on, with a CHECK that
     a consignor implies source='consigned' AND a commission.
     consignment_payables is a SIGNED ledger, not a mutable row:
     kind='accrual' when a consigned line sells, kind='reversal' when
     the sale voids or the line comes back. Balance = SUM(payable_amount)
     WHERE status='open'. Negative is a VALID state — a reversal landing
     after the consignor was paid means they owe the shop, and the next
     payout nets it off; mutating the accrual instead would silently
     erase a payment that actually happened. consignment_payouts records
     each cash-out, and consignment_pay_out() settles every open row in
     one transaction (refusing a zero or negative net). Staff RLS on
     payables and payouts is SELECT-ONLY — every write is a trigger or
     that RPC.

 372. THE ACCOUNTING IS TRIGGER-OWNED (0054). accrue on sales.status →
     'completed' (covers retail completion AND layaway payoff, because
     both flip the sale to 'completed'); reverse in full on → 'voided';
     reverse pro-rata on return_items INSERT. Gross is sale_items
     .line_total — what the line actually realized after any LINE
     discount; a cart-level discount (where loyalty redemption also
     lands) is the shop's own giveaway and is NOT prorated onto
     consigned lines. A separate BEFORE trigger on sale_items refuses
     any line whose EFFECTIVE per-unit price (line_total / quantity,
     i.e. after the line discount) falls below consignment_min_price —
     guarding unit_price alone would let a discount walk straight
     through the floor promised to the consignor. Same-tenant guards on
     consignors.customer_id and inventory_items.consignor_id.

 373. [BUG] CONSIGNMENT REVERSALS DOUBLE-COUNTED (found by the smoke
     test, fixed in patches/0055 before any shop had data). Root cause:
     consignment_reverse_on_void() reversed the FULL accrual for every
     line on the sale and consignment_reverse_on_return() reversed its
     pro-rata share of the FULL accrual — neither looked at reversals
     that had already landed. So a line fully returned (−160) and then
     voided (−160 again) put the consignor at −160 on an item that only
     ever accrued +160, and voidSaleAction IS reachable on a
     'fully_returned' sale. Repeated partial returns summing past the
     quantity sold had the same shape. 0055 adds
     consignment_unreversed_fraction() and both triggers now clamp:
     return reverses MIN(pro-rata, remainder), void reverses exactly the
     remainder, either skips when the remainder is already zero.
     Verified: three 1-unit returns against a 2-unit line followed by a
     void leave the line at exactly 0.00, never negative.

 374. CONSIGNMENT SURFACES. /consignors (list with live open balances +
     items on the floor, "owes shop" when negative), /consignors/new
     (customer picker + terms), /consignors/[id] (terms editor, items
     with agreement-expiry countdown in the SHOP's timezone and a
     return-to-consignor action, the full payable ledger naming
     Sold / Returned / Sale voided distinctly, payout history, and a
     payout dialog). Consignment fields appear on the inventory form
     only when source='consigned' and unmount otherwise — the Zod
     schema also CLEARS them server-side, because switching an item from
     consigned to bought without clearing would keep accruing a payable
     on goods the shop now owns. Commission is a FRACTION everywhere
     (column, schema, trigger); a shared CommissionField takes a percent
     and converts, so "20" can never become 2000%. /settings/consignment
     + a Consignors sidebar entry gated on settings.consignment_enabled.

 375. RULE 13 DELETE GATE EXTENDED. canDeleteCustomer() now also blocks
     on unspent store credit and on open consignment (goods still on the
     floor or an unsettled payable balance). Both are money the shop
     owes a person whose record would otherwise vanish. Reasons are
     translated EN+ES like the existing ones. CLAUDE.md rule 19 records
     both module conventions.

 376. VERIFICATION. Everything above was exercised against the LIVE
     database inside a DO block ending in a deliberate RAISE, so the
     whole transaction rolled back and nothing persisted (confirmed
     after: 0 consignors, 0 payables, 0 payouts, 0 credit events, both
     gates still FALSE). 26 assertions covering the sign CHECK, the
     append-only trigger, overdraw / overpay refusal, undo + double-undo,
     undo refused on a closed sale, void restore + idempotent re-restore,
     the coherence CHECK, the floor price with and without a line
     discount, no accrual before completion, no double accrual on
     re-completion, payout + "nothing payable", pro-rata partial return,
     the over-return clamp and the void clamp. tsc, eslint, 504 unit
     tests and the production build are all green.

 377. PRE-SAVE QUALITY GATE — 4 ORPHAN i18n KEYS. storeCredit.panel
     .viewAll and consignment.detail.terms were written and never wired
     (the terms heading reuses form.editTitle); both deleted from EN+ES.
     consignment.item.badge / .badgeOwed were written for a consigned-
     item badge that never got built — BUILT it instead of deleting:
     the inventory detail header now shows "On consignment" plus what
     the consignor takes home at the current price, via the shared
     splitConsignment() so the preview and the eventual accrual agree.
     That page previously said nothing about an item not being the
     shop's property, which is exactly what a clerk needs to know
     before quoting a price under a trigger-enforced floor.

 378. [BUG] CONSIGNMENT FIELDS LOST ON A VALIDATION ERROR.
     echoToInventoryFieldValues() in inventory/[id]/content.tsx never
     mapped the four consignment fields, so a failed save on the EDIT
     form silently reverted the clerk's typed consignor / commission /
     floor / expiry back to the DB values — the exact wipe the echo
     mechanism exists to prevent. (The NEW form was already correct.)
     Mapped them.

 379. DEAD CODE + DUPLICATE LOGIC SWEEP. Deleted five helpers written
     ahead of consumers that nothing called: readBalance,
     consignorBalance (the singular — the list uses the plural, the
     detail computes from the ledger it already has), sumDeltas,
     checkFloorPrice (the DB trigger is the authority; a second copy of
     that rule in dead TS is pure drift risk) and
     inventoryConsignmentSchema, plus their tests and two now-unused
     Zod helpers. Extracted src/lib/money.ts for r4 / r2 / toMoney,
     which this session had copy-pasted into TWO new modules;
     store-credit/math.ts and consignment/math.ts now re-export from
     it. FLAGGED, NOT TOUCHED: r4 / toMoney also sit duplicated in
     lib/pawn/math.ts, lib/pos/cart.ts and lib/repair/billing.ts —
     pre-existing, load-bearing on three shipped surfaces, and a
     repo-wide money refactor is the operator's call, not a gate fix.
     497 tests (down from 504 — the seven that only exercised deleted
     helpers went with them), lint / tsc / build green.

 364. [CRITICAL] BUY-OUTRIGHT NEVER REACHED THE POLICE REPORT (fixed in
     0048 / d636939). Root cause: compliance_log.source_table CHECK has
     allowed only ('loans','sales') since 0001, but the buy action
     inserts source_table='inventory_items' — and never checked the
     insert result, so every buy silently failed to log. Confirmed on
     the live DB: 1 bought item, 0 buy_outright compliance rows. CHECK
     widened; the action now checks the error and soft-deletes the
     just-inserted items rather than leave an unreported purchase.
     ALSO: canDeleteCustomer() existed but was never called (the Rule 13
     delete gate was dead) and matched buys on
     customer_snapshot->>customer_id when the snapshot key is id.
     Wired + fixed, now driven by jurisdictions.record_retention_years.

 365. COMMS AUTOMATIONS — CONFIGURABLE REMINDERS + LIFECYCLE MARKETING
     (patches/0049 + 0050, commit 906ce3d). 0049 is enum-only
     (loan_final_notice, birthday_greeting, dormant_winback,
     forfeiture_winback, redemption_thankyou) because Postgres refuses
     to use a new enum value inside the transaction that adds it — the
     rest lives in 0050. comm_automations = per-tenant on/off + day
     offset OVERRIDING code defaults (src/lib/comms/automations.ts,
     tested); comm_automation_sends = permanent send-once ledger keyed
     by a deterministic automation_key, replacing the old 24h
     message_log window that could double-send or block a retry.
     Catch-up window of 2 days, latest-step-wins after an outage.
     loan_final_notice is anchored to the STATUTORY forfeiture date
     from 0048. Lifecycle audience RPCs (birthday incl. Feb-29, dormant
     by last real transaction) are marketing-only: opt-in customers,
     7-day per-customer cap, off by default. New lifecycle-messages
     cron; maturity-reminders rewritten on the shared runner with
     shop-local dates. seed_default_message_templates now runs for
     EVERY tenant + on tenant insert — before this only one hard-coded
     tenant id had templates, so every future shop would have hit
     silent template_missing failures. The dispatcher gained the
     email <-> phone fallback its header always claimed, plus WhatsApp
     -> SMS fallback when no approved Content SID exists. The SMS
     opt-out footer is now bilingual.

 366. OFAC SDN SCREENING AT INTAKE (patches/0051, commit b19cca5).
     ofac_list_versions + ofac_sdn_names (individuals + aliases,
     pg_trgm GIN index) refreshed by a daily content-hashed cron off
     Treasury's SDN.CSV / ALT.CSV; first load 7,535 individuals /
     16,185 names (published 2026-09-17), ~15s, idempotent.
     ofac_candidates() does the index lookup (~12ms); scoring is pure
     TS (token-set Jaro-Winkler, 70% customer coverage + 30% SDN
     coverage) — the SDN-coverage term is what stops "Jose Garcia"
     from matching "GIL GARCIA, Jose Alejandro" while "Nicolas Maduro"
     still hits "MADURO MOROS, Nicolas". DOB-year conflicts are
     recorded but never block. ofac_screenings is append-only (only
     review fields change; FK cascades allowed via pg_trigger_depth).
     intakeGate() now blocks pawn + buy on a banned customer — which
     was NEVER enforced server-side before, only filtered out of the
     customer picker — plus unreviewed and confirmed matches. Owner /
     manager clears a false positive (documented reason) or confirms
     (which also bans). The screening id is stamped into the compliance
     snapshot.

--- Session 47 (2026-06-30, sixth pass) — Repair ticket multi-item redesign (line items + chip title-builder + per-item service) ---

 350. REPAIR_TICKET_LINE_ITEMS TABLE (patches/0046-repair-line-items.sql,
     NEW). A repair ticket now holds MULTIPLE customer items. New child
     table: id, ticket_id (FK CASCADE), tenant_id, line_index, item_type
     TEXT NOT NULL, karat TEXT, weight_grams NUMERIC(10,4) (metal-weight
     rule), dimension TEXT, title TEXT NOT NULL (composed), service_type
     service_type NOT NULL (PER ITEM), work_needed TEXT, created_at,
     deleted_at. Staff RLS (read + write) mirroring repair_ticket_stones
     (my_accessible_tenant_ids + my_is_staff). Indexes on (ticket_id,
     line_index) and (tenant_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL. LOCK
     IMMUTABILITY (rule #14): repair_line_items_enforce_lock() BEFORE
     UPDATE/DELETE raises when the parent repair_tickets.is_locked = TRUE
     (INSERT stays open, matching stones). BACKFILL: one line item per
     existing non-deleted ticket derived from its title / description /
     service_type (item_type='other'), idempotent via NOT EXISTS, so the
     detail page renders legacy tickets through the same path. NOTIFY
     pgrst + documented rollback. OPERATOR: apply 0046 then db:types.

 351. PURE COMPOSER + DERIVERS (src/lib/repair/line-items.ts, NEW) +
     11 vitest cases (line-items.test.ts, NEW). composeLineItemTitle
     builds "Necklace · 14k · 2.8g · 18\"" from canonical ENGLISH type
     labels (ITEM_TYPES) so stored titles are language-stable;
     normalizeWeightGrams trims trailing zeros / rejects <=0 / non-finite;
     deriveItemDescription joins "title — work" one line per item;
     deriveTicketTitle = first item + " (+N)" (capped 200). ITEM_TYPES
     (ring/necklace/bracelet/earrings/pendant/chain/watch/other) +
     KARAT_OPTIONS (10k/14k/18k/21k/22k/24k/925/950). Validation

     item_description / description (derived server-side instead).

 352. INTAKE FORM REWRITE (repair/new/form.tsx). Multi-item composer on
     the two-pane surface: each item is a LineItemEditor card with item-
     type chips, karat chips, typed weight(g) + length/size, per-item

 353. DETAIL PAGE LINE-ITEM RENDER (repair/[id]/page.tsx + content.tsx).
     page.tsx fetches repair_ticket_line_items via generic-client cast

--- Session 45 (2026-06-30, fourth pass) — Pawn Phase 2 step 1: Save-as-draft (loan_drafts) + dashboard redesign (pawn-dashboard.html) ---

 345. SAVE AS DRAFT — DEDICATED loan_drafts TABLE. Operator was
     asked (genuine regulated-schema fork) how to store drafts; chose
     a separate table over (a) adding 'draft' to the loan_status enum +
     relaxing the loans CHECK constraints + skipping the ticket-number
     trigger, or (b) keeping all terms required. ROOT REASONING: the
     loans table enforces principal>0 / term_days 1-365 / due_date NOT
     NULL at the DB level and a BEFORE-INSERT trigger burns a
     sequential PT-NNNNNN number on every insert — so a "customer-only"
     draft is impossible without altering a regulated financial table
     and consuming a ticket number auditors expect to be contiguous.
     patches/0045-loan-drafts.sql (NEW): loan_drafts (id, tenant_id FK,
     customer_id FK NOT NULL, payload JSONB DEFAULT '{}', created/
     updated_by, created/updated_at, deleted_at), idx on (tenant_id,
     updated_at desc) + (customer_id), update_updated_at trigger, RLS
     ENABLE + loan_drafts_staff_read/_write mirroring the loans
     policies exactly (NEW table/NEW policies — no existing isolation
     altered), NOTIFY pgrst. OPERATOR: apply 0045 then `npm run
     db:types`. Until then loan_drafts is reached via a generically-
     typed client (`supabase as unknown as SupabaseClient`) —
     documented, drops to typed access post-db:types.

 346. DRAFT ACTIONS + LIST + RESUME + FINALIZE-DELETE. New
     src/app/(staff)/pawn/drafts/actions.ts: saveLoanDraft(formData)
     (gate has_pawn + requireRoleInTenant owner/manager/pawn_clerk/
     chain_admin; require customer_id [the one hard requirement];
     defense-in-depth customer-belongs-to-tenant check; buildDraftPayload
     -> insert or update by draft_id; audit create/update) and
     deleteLoanDraft(formData) (soft-delete via deleted_at; audit
     soft_delete) — both redirect-style (formData)=>Promise<void>, not
     useActionState. New /pawn/drafts page.tsx (loads drafts via
     generic client + parseDraftPayload -> DraftRow[]) + content.tsx
     (table: customer / items / principal / saved-at; Continue ->
     /pawn/new?draft=id; Discard -> deleteLoanDraft form). /pawn/new
     page.tsx reads ?draft= and loads the row -> initialDraft prop.
     form.tsx: seeds principal/termDays/rateChoice/notes + CustomerPicker
     initialCustomerId from the draft, injects collateral rows once on
     mount via collateralRef.addExtractedRow (photos NOT restored —
     not staged; category slugs NOT restored — addExtractedRow
     limitation, same as voice intake), hidden draft_id + rate_id
     inputs, and a rail "Save as draft" button (useTransition; builds
     FormData from the form element; disabled until a customer is
     picked). createLoanAction reads draft_id and soft-deletes the
     draft after a successful issue (before redirect). /pawn list
     gained a "Drafts" link.

 347. SHARED INTAKE PARSER — src/lib/pawn/intake-form.ts (NEW,
     non-'use server' so multiple action files can import its sync

 348. DASHBOARD REDESIGN (from operator mockup pawn-dashboard.html).
