--- Session 63 (2026-11-22) — Layaway terms, sales tax by district + per-item cap, card reader tips, customer popup + validation (branch feature/sol-pawn-brief == main) ---

 520. LOYALTY SETTINGS PAGE TRANSLATED (7e22e6e).
      src/app/(staff)/settings/loyalty/content.tsx reads every string
      from a new loyalty.settings block (EN + ES). actions.ts returns
      error CODES ('validation_failed' | 'save_failed') instead of the
      raw database message, so the error line is translated too; the
      Save button shows "Saving…".

 521. SETTINGS FORMS RESET AFTER A SAVE [BUG] (4a3a441).
      Root cause: React resets a <form action={fn}> when the action
      finishes, but component state stays. On Loyalty, Store credit and
      Consignment the "turn on" checkbox is held in state, so after a
      save it SHOWED unticked while the page still treated it as on — a
      second Save would have switched the module off.
      Fix: new src/lib/ui/submit-keeping-values.ts (onSubmit +
      startTransition, no reset), used by the three forms. Browser-
      checked on Loyalty and Store credit with a rejected save;
      Consignment has the same line but was not submitted (every value
      it accepts would have saved).

 522. LAYAWAY TERMS (633f2a5, patches/0093 APPLIED).
      Eddy: most shops write every layaway on the same terms.
      - Table layaway_terms (name, schedule_kind, down_payment_pct,
        first_payment_days, term_days, cancellation_fee_pct, is_default,
        is_active, position). One default per shop (unique index). RLS
        approved by Eddy: staff read, owner + manager write.
      - src/lib/pos/layaway-terms.ts: schema (percent typed, fraction
        stored), applyLayawayTerm(term, total, today) → dates counted
        from the shop's today (todayInTimezone), minimum down payment.
      - Settings → Layaway terms: add / edit / reorder / default / remove.
        The first plan added becomes the default.
      - src/components/pos/LayawayScheduleDialog.tsx: a Terms list
        (default picked), a read-only summary, the down payment pre-
        filled at the plan's share (more is allowed, less is refused),
        and "Custom" = the original fields. No plans = the old dialog.
      - layaways and layaway_create() are unchanged: the dialog sends
        the same fields. The audit log records which terms were used
        (layaway_term_id → name).

 523. QUICK "NEW CUSTOMER" POPUP COULD NOT SAVE ANYONE [BUG] (c33555e).
      Found while wiring it into the sale form. Root cause:
      createCustomerFromForm passed formData.get() straight to the
      schema; the popup has no country / id_country / comm_preference /
      language field, so those arrived as NULL, and zod's .default()
      only fills UNDEFINED. Every save was refused, with no message,
      because the popup shows errors only under fields it has. Affected
      the pawn, repair and quote forms too.
      Fix: src/lib/customers/create.ts sends a missing field as
      undefined. The popup also keeps what was typed after a refused
      save (submitKeepingValues). Browser-checked on the sale form only.

 524. SALES TAX BY DISTRICT + CUSTOMER "+" ON THE SALE FORM (1fc3e09,
      patches/0094 APPLIED).
      - Table tax_rates (name, level state|county|city|district|other,
        rate numeric(6,4), is_active). RLS approved by Eddy with
        MANAGERS able to edit: staff read, owner + manager write.
      - Trigger trg_tax_rates_sync_total (SECURITY DEFINER — managers
        cannot write settings) keeps settings.default_tax_rate = sum of
        the active rows; refuses a stack over 100%. Nothing else writes
        that column now (CLAUDE.md rule 20).
      - Settings → Sales tax; hub card shows the total. A rate finer
        than two decimals of a percent is refused, not rounded.
      - New sale / layaway: the Tax field starts at the shop's total and
        shows what it is made of; the clerk can still change it.
      - Online store settings show the rate and link to Sales tax
        (toSettingsPatch and tax_rate_pct removed).
      - Cart.tsx: "+" beside the customer search opens
        QuickCustomerModal; the new customer goes onto the sale.
      - The Open layaway button states the amount it will collect.

 525. LAYAWAY L-000002: "TYPED 150, CHARGED 50" — NOT REPRODUCED.
      Audit log + rows show the server stored exactly what the browser
      sent (down_payment 50). 12 ways of changing the box to 150 on the
      LIVE site (request captured and aborted) all sent 150. Eddy said
      to ignore it. L-000002 was not touched (paid $50, balance $150).
      The button label from 524 is the safeguard.

 526. CUSTOMER PHONE + EMAIL VALIDATED (46740a9).
      Eddy's screenshot: phone "dfdd", email "fds". Phone was
      optionalTrimmedString — anything saved. New
      src/lib/customers/phone.ts normalizePhone(): digits + the usual
      separators, 7-15 digits; 10 digits (or 11 starting with 1) is

      rule). Error codes 'invalid_phone' / 'invalid_email' translated by

 527. RECEIPT BALANCE LARGE (ccd8d03).
      balanceOwed() in src/lib/pos/receipt-thermal.ts: what is still

 528. LAYAWAY LIST "DUE SOON" STUCK ON EVERY TAB [BUG] (ccd8d03).
      Eddy: "when i click active again it doesnt bring up the active".
      Root cause: every status chip built its link with the current due

 529. PER-ITEM TAX CAP, ANY STATE (09a0640, patches/0095 APPLIED).
      Eddy: "add a cap but not just for florida".
      - tax_rates.item_cap: "only the first $X of each item", per unit.
        NULL = the whole price. No state's figure is in the code.
      - sales_tax_quote(tenant, qty[], net[]): ONE function computes a
        sale's tax. Tax is rounded ONCE on the whole sale; the split by
        tax line and by sale line is reconciled to the cent (leftover
        cent to the largest).
      - sale_create / layaway_create gain p_tax_mode (default 'flat', so
        old callers are unchanged): 'shop' = the shop's tax lines, caps
        included, cart discount spread over the lines first; 'flat' =
        the rate the clerk typed. online_order_reserve uses the same
        function. Bodies were generated from the LIVE definitions.
      - New: sales.tax_breakdown (tax per tax line) and
        sale_items.tax_amount (tax per line). Returns refund the line's
        own tax when present (src/lib/pos/returns.ts).
      - src/lib/pos/sales-tax.ts quoteSaleTax / computeCartTotals: the
        same arithmetic in whole cents (BigInt for the discount split);
        tests are pinned to the numbers the database produced, plus 400
        random carts proving "no cap" equals the old formula.
      - Verified: rolled-back dry run (sale, layaway, online order), unit
        tests, sale screen in a browser ($10,000 item → $10,675.00, not
        $10,750.00). See RESUME POINT (a) for what was not.

 530. CARD READER TIPS, CREDITED TO THE EMPLOYEE (a43607a,
      patches/0096 APPLIED). Eddy: like Luna Azul, with a switch, and
      the tip saved to the proper user.
      - Read Luna Azul's version READ-ONLY for reference (a switch +
        skip_tipping; it does not credit an employee). No file there was
        touched.
      - settings.reader_tips_enabled / reader_tip_percentages /
        stripe_terminal_tip_config_id; sale_payments.tip_amount /
        tip_user_id; sale_card_payment_set_tip() is the only writer and
        records a tip once (CLAUDE.md rule 21).
      - processPaymentIntentOnReader({ askForTip }): off → skip_tipping
        (as before); on → the reader offers the shop's choices, kept in
        a Stripe Terminal Configuration by ensureReaderTipChoices().
      - [BUG avoided] the webhook refused any card tender whose charged
        amount differed from the tender; a tipped charge would never
        have settled there. It now expects tender + the tip Stripe

 482. LIVE METALS AUTO-REFRESH. The dashboard used to show the price from
     page load until a reload. Now: lib/spot-prices/strip.ts holds
     loadMetalsStrip + loadDisplayPct (moved out of dashboard/page.tsx,
     unchanged) and MetalStripItem / MetalsStripData. GET /api/spot/strip
     (requireStaff, user client for the tenant's display percent, no-store)
     returns the same data. components/dashboard/useLiveMetals.ts polls it
     every 2 min while the tab is visible, stops when hidden, and reads at
     once when the tab comes back; a failed read keeps the last numbers.
     Reads saved prices only — never calls the provider (the cron does:
     every 15 min weekdays 8:00-4:45 ET, hourly otherwise). lookup.ts
     still caches 5 min in-process, so worst case ≈ refresh + 5 + 2 min.
     Verified in the browser: display percent changed in a second tab,
     dashboard tab updated 90% → 80% ($3,185.10 → $2,831.20) without a
     reload; signed-out request → 401; percent restored to 90.

 483. [CRITICAL] EVERY COUNTER SALE AND LAYAWAY FAILED SINCE 0076
     (2026-11-13) — FIXED (patches/0084 APPLIED, no policy change).
     Eddy: Charge (cash and card) → "Something went wrong. Please retry."
     Production log: "[pos.money] sale_create DELETE requires a WHERE
     clause". ROOT CAUSE: Supabase loads pg_safeupdate for API
     (PostgREST) requests; it refuses DELETE / UPDATE with no WHERE even
     on a temp table. sale_create cleared AND priced its temp line table
     that way (two statements). The 0076 dry run passed because the
     Management API SQL session (scripts/apply-migration.mjs) does NOT
     load safeupdate — a dry run there cannot catch this class of bug.
     0084 = the live function body with those two statements given
     WHERE true, nothing else. Proven through the API (service-role rpc,
     same piece twice): before → "DELETE requires a WHERE clause", after
     → duplicate_item (the intended refusal, rolled back, no sale).
     Scanned all 119 live public functions: no other UPDATE/DELETE
     without WHERE. A full Charge was NOT run (it would write a real
     sale) — Eddy retries at the counter.
     ALSO: createSaleAction returned 'validation_failed' / 'not_authorized'
     but the keys are validationFailed / notAuthorized, so those showed the
     generic message too. Renamed.
     LESSON: test any new SQL function through supabase-js .rpc(), not
     only through apply-migration.mjs.

 484. OUT OF STOCK — "ADD ANYWAY" FOR OWNER / CHAIN ADMIN / MANAGER
     (OUT_OF_STOCK_ADD_ROLES in lib/pos/quick-sale.ts). In the "already in
     cart, none in stock" popup, and on a product quick sale button with
     zero stock (now tappable for those roles, asks first: "Out of
     stock"), "Add anyway" adds unlinkedLine(): a custom line with the

     one piece; sale_create refuses a piece twice). Price: the scanned
     item's list price, or the button's last unit / saved reference price
     (product buttons now carry price). Clerks see the old OK-only popup.
     No server change: any clerk can already type a custom line.
     Verified in the browser as owner: double scan → Add anyway → 2 lines,
     $400 total; zero-stock button enabled and asks; no console errors.

 485. CHARGE WITH CASH COMPLETES THE SALE; STAY ON NEW SALE. The Cash/Card
     choice used to be UI-only (Charge made an open sale and redirected).
     createSaleAction now takes the tender: cash → sale_add_payment +
     sale_complete + afterSaleCompleted in the same request, then the form

 492. CARD REFUNDS NEVER FAIL SILENTLY + VOID REASON ON THE SALE.
     Before: void and return refunded Stripe best effort — a refused refund
     was only console.error'd and the sale still showed voided / returned.
     Now (lib/pos/card-refund.ts pure planner + 5 tests;
     card-refund-server.ts loadRefundableCharges + runCardRefunds):
     - remaining per charge = Stripe amount - amount_refunded (partial
       returns count); newest charge first; idempotency key per
       void/return + payment + cents, so a retry never double-refunds.
     - VOID refunds FIRST; any failure: sale NOT voided, error
       cardRefundFailed with Stripe's message, audit card_refund_failed;
       pressing Void again retries only what is left. Charges already at
       zero are marked refunded.
     - RETURN with method card refunds BEFORE writing the return (id made
       up front = idempotency key). Fails: nothing recorded. Stripe covers
       less than the return: cardRefundShort (says how much it can).
       Card-machine-only sale: recorded as a manual refund
       (card_refund_manual in audit). Fails part-way: return recorded with
       refunded_total = what Stripe refunded + cardRefundPartial warning.
     - Void popup + return form say card-machine payments are refunded on
       the machine. Void popup now TRANSLATES errors (showed raw codes).
     patches/0088 APPLIED (no policy change): sales.void_reason /
     voided_at / voided_by, write-once trigger, backfilled from audit_log
     (S-000007). Sale page red banner: Voided by WHO, time, reason.
     ClientTime moved to components/ClientTime.tsx (shared with the laser
     panel) so the stamp never causes a hydration mismatch.
     Eddy voided S-000007 on the OLD code: Stripe refund succeeded (row
     shows Refunded). The new refund paths are NOT yet exercised live.

 455. LASER ORDERS — SCHEMA (patches/0070, APPLIED). Enum
     laser_order_status (new / received / cut / done / rejected /
     cancelled). Table laser_orders: one row per order SENT (created only
     once orocut.com answered with an order_id) — tenant_id,
     repair_ticket_id (RESTRICT), external_id = ticket_number,
     orocut_order_id (UNIQUE — the webhook resolves the tenant from it),
     the payload as sent (text, text2, style, quantity, metal,
     thickness_mm, chain_length_in, height_mm, customer_name, due_at,
     notes, picture_url), what came back (reject_reason, cut_details
     JSONB, cut_count, status_history JSONB [{status, at, via, by,
     reason}], remote_updated_at, last_synced_at, last_sync_error),
     sent_by/sent_at/finished_at, soft-delete. Partial unique index: ONE
     open order per ticket. Trigger: the payload as sent is immutable
     and a final status never moves. RLS mirrors repair_tickets.
     settings.orocut_style_names TEXT[] (the shop's OroCut style names,
     typed by hand). The order token + webhook secret are vault kinds
     orocut_order_token / orocut_webhook_secret — never a column.
     DESIGN: the order hangs off the REPAIR TICKET, not the custom quote
     — the quote is terminal once converted; the ticket is the thing
     being made (number, due date, customer).

     (PURE): mergeRemoteStatus(row, remote, via) — a remote observation
     counts only when NEWER than remote_updated_at (webhook retries and
     stale polls change nothing), same status again is a no-op except
     `cut` which bumps cut_count, rejected keeps the reason, final
     stamps finished_at; 10 tests. signature.ts: X-OroCut-Signature
     sha256=<hex|base64 HMAC> constant-time; 3 tests. client.ts
     (server-only): createOrder (409 → conflict + existing order_id),
     getOrder (tolerant parser incl. history), cancelOrder (409 →
     refused + current status), probeToken (GET ?external_id=… — 401 vs
     404 tells a bad token from a good one); base URL OROCUT_API_BASE
     (platform env, mock/staging) or https://orocut.com; 15 s timeout;
     the token never logs. sync.ts: applyRemoteStatus (merge + update +
     audit laser_order_status/cancel + a "Laser: …" note on the ticket

     last_sync_error, never thrown).

     roles → ticket not closed → no open order → token → POST →
     insert; 409 with an unknown order_id is ADOPTED then synced;
     callback_url only when the webhook secret is saved AND APP_URL is
     https), cancelLaserOrderAction (POST /cancel; 409 → sync + error
     cancel_refused with the real status), refreshLaserOrderAction.
     /api/webhooks/orocut: resolve row by order_id → tenant's webhook
     secret → verify → merge; unknown order 404 (orocut.com retries),
     bad signature 401. /api/cron/sync-laser-orders (*/5, CRON_SECRET):
     open orders not checked in 4 min, 100 per run, one token lookup per
     tenant. /settings/integrations/orocut (owner/chain_admin): order
     token + webhook secret (write-only, format-checked, clear checkbox),
     style names one per line (dedup, ≤100, ≤80 chars), webhook URL
     shown, Test token. Integrations index card (open orders + style
     count). Audit: laser_order_send / _cancel / _status,
     orocut_settings. i18n repair.laser.* + orocut.* EN+ES.

 458. LIVE API CHECK. Eddy said the orders API is live (Tocoloro
     session); the two handoff .md files are not on this disk, so the
     contract was read from Para Cuba Solutions src/lib/licensing/
     {orders,orders-api}.ts (read-only, other lane). Differences from
     the spec that changed the build: (1) webhooks are signed with a
     SEPARATE whsec_+48hex secret issued beside the token, not the token
     → second vault kind + settings field; without it we send no
     callback_url and rely on polling. (2) token is ord_+40hex → regex
     field check. (3) envelope {ok, …}; 409 duplicate_external_id
     carries order_id + status; cancel 409 invalid_transition carries
     status; GET returns orderJson + history [{status, by, reason,
     details, at}] — all already parsed. (4) create body is .strict():
     we send only known keys and omit empties. (5) callback host must
     be https, non-private, with a dot — Vercel prod URL qualifies.

--- Session 56 (2026-11-04/05) — Stripe Connect per-tenant Express onboarding (branch feature/sol-pawn-brief, dispatched by the Manager session) ---

 449. STRIPE CONNECT — WHY AND WHICH ACCOUNT TYPE. Settings → Integrations
     linked to /api/stripe/connect/start but only webhook/ and saas/*
     existed under api/stripe/; lib/stripe/terminal.ts and
     payment-link.ts both throw tenant_stripe_not_connected when
     tenant_billing_settings.stripe_account_id is null, and nothing ever
     wrote it. CLAUDE.md says "same OAuth flow as Abacus" (Standard
     accounts, STRIPE_CONNECT_CLIENT_ID) but the ask was Account Links +
     "Open Stripe dashboard", and login links exist ONLY for Express
     accounts — so: EXPRESS. Terminal and Checkout already act on the
     connected account via the platform STRIPE_SECRET_KEY + Stripe-
     Account header, which is exactly how Express works, so neither
     surface changed. No OAuth client id, no per-tenant access/refresh
     tokens (0034 already dropped those columns), no per-account webhook
     provisioning (Abacus did that; here ONE platform Connect webhook
     receives every tenant's events). No `stripe` npm package — same
     REST-over-fetch style as the three existing lib/stripe modules.

 450. ROUTES. All three are GET route handlers (runtime nodejs, force-
     dynamic) behind lib/stripe/connect-guard.ts: getCtx() → /login when
     signed out, /no-tenant without an active tenant, ?stripe=forbidden
     unless tenantRole is owner | chain_admin (the same OWNER_ROLES the
     Integrations card uses), ?stripe=misconfigured without
     NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL. STRIPE_SECRET_KEY missing → misconfigured too.
     - /api/stripe/connect/start: loadTenantBilling → reuse
       stripe_account_id or POST /accounts (type=express, country US,
       card_payments + transfers requested, business_profile.name = dba
       ?? name, email, metadata.tenant_id) with Idempotency-Key
       pawn_connect_account_<tenantId> so a double click or a failed
       save-then-retry cannot mint two accounts; upsert the id on
       tenant_billing_settings; audit stripe_connect_account_create;
       POST /account_links (type=account_onboarding, return_url =
       /api/stripe/connect/callback, refresh_url = /api/stripe/connect/
       start) and 302 to Stripe.
     - /api/stripe/connect/callback: the return_url carries NO proof of
       completion, so GET /accounts/{id} and write the truth: syncAccount
       Status() → stripe_charges_enabled / stripe_payouts_enabled /
       stripe_details_submitted / stripe_status_synced_at,
       billing_enabled = charges_enabled (payment-link.ts's
       isTenantStripeConnected and the POS gate read it), and
       stripe_connected_at stamped ONCE the first time the account is
       fully connected (never moved, never cleared). Audit stripe_connect
       _status_sync with requirements.currently_due + disabled_reason so
       /audit shows WHY an account is stuck. Redirect ?stripe=connected
       or ?stripe=incomplete.
     - /api/stripe/connect/dashboard: POST /accounts/{id}/login_links and
       302; not_connected → back to settings; incomplete → into /start.
     Error paths each log with a [stripe-connect/<route>] prefix and
     redirect ?stripe=stripe_error (Stripe refused) or save_failed (DB).
     Links on the card are plain <a>, not <Link> — a client-side
     navigation into a route handler that 302s off-site would try to
     fetch an RSC payload from stripe.com.

 451. WEBHOOK account.updated. /api/stripe/webhook (the Connect endpoint,
     STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET) now routes account.updated by
     stripe_account_id → syncAccountStatus; audits only when one of the
     three flags actually changed (status_before/after + requirements);
     unknown accounts (the platform's own, stale test accounts) are
     logged and ignored. This is what flips a shop to Connected hours
     later when Stripe finishes bank verification, and what turns
     billing_enabled OFF if Stripe disables charges. The endpoint must
     be registered in the Dashboard as "Listen to events on Connected
     accounts" or account.updated never arrives (item 454).

 452. SETTINGS → INTEGRATIONS + i18n. page.tsx derives status with
     deriveConnectStatus() (connected = details_submitted AND
     charges_enabled; payouts pending does not block taking cards),
     reads ?stripe= through an allowlist, and passes only the account
     id's LAST 4 to the client. content.tsx: pill Not connected /
     Onboarding incomplete (new warning-tone 'incomplete' pill) /
     Connected; rows account •••• last4, connected date, charges,
     payouts, terminal location, last checked; one action per state
     (Connect with Stripe / Continue onboarding / Open Stripe dashboard),
     owner-only otherwise; a banner for every ?stripe= flag. The hub
     (/settings) "connected" flag uses the same derivation instead of
     stripe_connected_at. New dictionary section stripeConnect.* in BOTH
     en.ts and es.ts (es is typed Dictionary, so tsc IS the parity gate
     — there is no .githooks/pre-push in this repo despite the en.ts
     header comment). The rest of the integrations page stays hardcoded
     English (pre-existing Rule 6 debt, out of scope).

 453. MIGRATION 0069 (NOT APPLIED) + PURE MODULE + GATE FIX.
     patches/0069-stripe-connect-status.sql: three NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE
     booleans + stripe_status_synced_at + a UNIQUE partial index on
     stripe_account_id (one account ↔ one tenant; the webhook routes by
     it). src/types/database.ts hand-edited for the four columns (item
     245 precedent) — `npm run db:types` after applying must produce the
     same shape. lib/stripe/connect-status.ts is pure (deriveConnect
     Status, accountIdSuffix, normalizeAppUrl, buildAccountLinkUrls,
     buildSettingsRedirect, flagForStatus, billingPatchFromAccount) with
     17 vitest cases. Pre-save gate: stripeFetch/encodeForm/
     stripeSecretKey existed as three private copies in terminal.ts,
     payment-link.ts and saas.ts and this work was about to add a
     fourth — consolidated into src/lib/stripe/http.ts (GET/POST/DELETE,
     optional Stripe-Account + Idempotency-Key, null/undefined body
     values skipped — a superset of all three, byte-identical requests
     for existing callers). Dead view field detailsSubmitted removed.

 454. VERIFIED / NOT VERIFIED / WHAT EDDY DOES. Verified: tsc --noEmit,
     eslint, vitest 655/655 (32 files), next build green with the three

          api/stripe/webhook → "Listen to: Events on Connected accounts"

          payment_intent.succeeded → copy its signing secret.
       4. Vercel env (Production + Preview): STRIPE_SECRET_KEY (sk_test_
          first), STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET = the secret from step 3 (the

          fill the hosted form with Stripe's test values (phone

          000123456789) → return → card reads Connected, charges enabled

          stripe_connect_status_sync.
       6. Local alternative: NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=http://localhost:3060

--- Session 55 (2026-11-03/04) — Brief Phase 0 (spot prices) + Phase 1 (tracking link) + Phase 2 (custom quoter) + Phase 3 (metal counter) + Phase 4 (smaller items) ---

 PHASE 4 (items 441-446) — "smaller items, one summary each".

 441. 4.1 BENCH CALCULATORS — /tools (sidebar "Bench tools", any staff
     role). Six pure calculators in lib/bench/{ring-size,weight,alloy}.ts
     with a "How this is calculated" note under each and 16 unit tests:
     ring size (US/CA/MX — Mexico uses the US scale, verified; EU = ISO
     8653 circumference; Japan approx. 13.0 mm + 1/3 mm per size, SAID to
     be approximate in the UI; inside mm), weight units (g/dwt/ozt/
     avoirdupois oz, exact constants), alloy down (W·(Kh/Kt − 1)), raise
     karat (W·(Kt − Kl)/(24 − Kt), 24K refused), mix lots (Σwk/Σw), and a
     melt/payout quick calc off today's spot × pay rate (+ sell price when
     a sell rate is set) reusing lib/metal-counter/math. The ONLY write:
     per-user "recent calculations" (patches/0066 bench_calculations,
     own-rows RLS, hard delete allowed — scratch paper, not a ledger).
     lib/bench/combos.ts is the client-safe twin of COUNTER_COMBOS.

 442. 4.2 INVENTORY FIELDS (patches/0067, APPLIED). inventory_items +=
     condition (new/used/restored/vintage/antique), coating (none /
     gold_plated / gold_filled / vermeil / pvd, NOT NULL default none),
     finish (free text + datalist suggestions), shelf_location,
     legacy_code (indexed, searchable), wholesale_price. All on the item
     form (InventoryFormFields), the list (condition + coating filters,
     coated badge, legacy/shelf in the subtitle, legacy_code in search),
     create/update actions and the detail page. loan_collateral_items +=
     coating so the pawn ticket records it.

 443. 4.2 COATING EXCLUDES FROM MELT — the rule with teeth. lib/spot-
     prices/purity.ts isSolidMetal(coating); gated at every melt site:
     inventory detail resolveMelt (panel replaced by the warning), buy
     outright (rateForRow → no rate, no auto payout, amber line; action
     skips the melt snapshot; stale gate ignores coated items), pawn
     intake (CollateralItemsList coating select + warning; intake-form
     reads it; stale gate; suggested-loan tryComputeMelt pushes
     'coated_not_solid_metal' and returns null). Legacy rows (NULL) count
     as solid.

 444. 4.2 AUTO SKU + SUGGEST PRICE. inventory_items_assign_sku() now
     builds <CAT>-<METAL>-<seq> (RNG-AU-000123, ELC-000124 when the metal
     is none/other/NULL) from the SAME per-tenant counter, so old INV-
     SKUs keep their numbers; an app-provided SKU still wins. Verified
     with a rolled-back insert. "Suggest price" on the item form = cost ÷
     (1 − margin) via the quoter's priceFromCost; the margin is
     settings.inventory_target_margin_pct (default 50%), editable on
     Settings → Tenant info ("Inventory pricing"). lib/inventory/
     pricing.ts loads it.

     TODAY via the existing payoffFromLoan — interest math untouched;
     late = days past due_date), active layaways (late = days since the
     first installment NOT covered by payments — lib/collections/aging
     derives the installment dates from schedule_kind + first/final due;
     'custom'/undated layaways are judged on the final date), repair

     promised_date or picked_up_at). Buckets On time / Slightly late /
     Very late / Urgent with the edges in settings.collections_slight_
     days / _very_days (patches/0068, APPLIED; DB CHECK very > slight;
     editable on Settings → Tenant info). KPIs: total owed, % overdue,
     average days to collect (days past due at payment, redemptions +
     completed layaways, 90-day lookback), % promised payments kept
     (layaway installments due so far that are covered), top 5 customers.
     Row actions: tel: link, SendReminderDialog (existing manualSendAction,
     kinds per source), "Record payment" → the existing detail page. 10
     unit tests on the pure math. Definitions are spelled out in the
     page footnote because the brief left them open.

     .999 / platinum / palladium per gram, % vs the oldest point in the

 447. PRODUCTION BUG — /tools "Keep this" → "This page couldn't load"
     (Eddy, 2026-11-04). Root cause: tools/actions.ts ('use server')
     exported the CALCULATORS const beside the two actions. Next 16
     allows ONLY async-function exports from a 'use server' module and
     throws "A "use server" file can only export async functions, found
     object" when an action is invoked — the GET renders fine, so the
     route smoke (navigation only) never caught it; the POST was the
     first evaluation of the actions loader. Fix (3ba0aa1): CALCULATORS
     + CalculatorKind moved to src/lib/bench/calculators.ts. Swept every
     other 'use server' file — none exports a non-async value (type
     exports are fine, they erase). Reproduced + verified with a
     headless click script (scratchpad repro-keep.mjs: smoke-style
     login, open /tools, click Keep this): before = POST /tools 500 +
     error page, after = "Saved to your recent calculations." The one
     row the repro wrote was deleted afterwards. RULE: a 'use server'
     file exports async functions only — constants and enums go in lib/.
     LESSON: the browser smoke only navigates; every new server action
     needs at least one real invocation before it ships.

 448. PRODUCTION BUG — Buy outright submit → "This page couldn't load"
     (found by driving every Phase 3/4 action in production after item
     447, scratchpad drive-actions-prod.mjs). buy/new/actions.ts ended
     with `export type { BuyItemInput }` (there since dd33b78,
     2026-04-28). Turbopack's 'use server' transform keeps that
     re-export as a runtime reference → "ReferenceError: BuyItemInput is
     not defined" at module evaluation of the actions loader → every
     createBuyOutrightAction call 500s. Nothing imported the re-export.
     Fix: removed the re-export + the unused type import. Verified
     locally: empty submit now returns "Please fix the highlighted
     fields below." instead of the 500. Swept every 'use server' file —
     no other `export {`/`export type {`/`export *`. RULE (with 447): a
     'use server' file contains async functions and `export type Foo =`
     declarations ONLY — no consts, no re-exports of any kind.
     Driven in production, same session, all OK: settings/general save
     ("Saved."), spot sell-rate save ("Save ✓"), inventory create +
     update (validation path), sell metal (validation path), buy
     outright (after this fix). A real metal sale / mixed-tender buy
     with money still needs Eddy's hands.

 PHASE 3 (items 432-440) — two-way metal counter, buy AND sell.

 432. PHASE 3 DESIGN DECISION: a metal SALE is a `sales` row. Brief says
     a sell must hit the audit log and the reports but never the police
     report. The register close and the sales summary already read
     sales / sale_items / sale_payments, so a scrap or bullion sale is
     persisted as sale_kind='metal' (0064), status completed + locked at
     insert (money changed hands at the counter), with ONE sale_items row
     per metal line and ONE sale_payments row per tender. No
     compliance_log write anywhere on that path. Alternative (a separate
     metal_sales table) would have needed its own register + report
     plumbing and a second void/return story. The receipt lives at
     /buy/sell/[id] (not under /pos) so a pawn-only shop without the
     retail module still sees what it sold; "Open in POS" appears when
     has_retail.

 433. MIGRATIONS 0064 + 0065 (APPLIED 2026-11-04). 0064 (own file, same
     enum-before-use split as 0052/0060): payment_method += 'transfer',
     sale_kind += 'metal'. 0065: spot_price_overrides.sell_multiplier
     NUMERIC(6,4) NULL (0..5) — the SELL rate as a sibling column, not a
     second meaning of `multiplier`; sale_items gets metal_type, purity,
     weight_grams, spot_per_gram, rate_multiplier (NULL on retail lines);
     sales.counterparty_name TEXT for a refiner who is not a customer.
     Verified by SELECT; db:types regenerated.

 434. PURE LIBS. lib/spot-prices/purity.ts — purityFromItem /
     meltMetalFromItem moved out of melt.ts (which imports the admin
     client) so client forms stop carrying their own copies; melt.ts
     re-exports. lib/metal-counter/math.ts — resolveRate(maps, metal,
     purity, 'pay'|'sell') (sell unset → multiplier 1 + configured=false),
     dollarsFromGrams / gramsFromDollars, totalsByPurity (gold first,
     highest purity first), splitTenders ('mixed' → cash + transfer rows
     that must sum to the cent; zero parts dropped because
     sale_payments.amount > 0). 30 new tests (612 total).
     lib/metal-counter/rates.ts (server) builds the plain RateMaps the
     forms hold: spot + pay + sell per `${metal}::${purity}`.

 435. BUY OUTRIGHT — TWO-WAY ENTRY. Each item has an "Enter by: Weight |
     Amount" toggle. Weight mode = today's behaviour (grams → payout at
     the pay rate; a hand-typed payout is a negotiation and sticks until
     the grams are retyped). Amount mode = the brief's "customer wants
     $300 for this 14K — how many grams?": dollars → grams, weight box
     read-only. Derivation runs in the change handlers (derive()), not in
     a render-time sync, so no eslint-disable. Rate line under each item
     shows "$X/g = spot $Y/g × pay rate Z%". Totals: melt, grams, payout
     + a per-(metal, purity) breakdown (MetalTotalsTable). Payout
     methods: cash, transfer, MIXED (cash + transfer with live
     "still to split" hint), check, card, other, store credit. The buy
     has no tender ledger, so the split is validated server-side and
     recorded on the audit row (`tenders`, `total_grams`). The whole
     form is now i18n'd (it was hardcoded English; buy.form.*).

 436. SELL METAL — /buy/sell (page, form, actions, persist). Gated
     has_pawn OR has_retail; roles owner/manager/pawn_clerk/chain_admin.
     Lines: metal (4 spot metals) + purity (buckets per metal) + grams ⇄
     price at the SELL rate with the same Weight|Amount toggle;
     description auto-fills "Gold 14k scrap" until edited; "Add from
     inventory" pulls an available precious-metal item onto a line
     (metal/karat/weight prefilled) and the sale flips it to 'sold'.
     Unset sell rate → amber "pricing at spot (100%) — set one on Spot
     prices" (never silent). Buyer = customer picker and/or free-text
     refiner name (one required). Tax rate % (default 0, with the
     refiner-exempt note). Tenders: cash, transfer, mixed, check (card
     left out: Terminal isn't wired and a "completed" sale with a
     pending card would lie). Attaches the open register session when
     there is one and SAYS which case applies. Stale-spot gate applies.
     persist.ts: sale → lines (spot_per_gram + rate_multiplier frozen
     from the live feed at submit) → payments → inventory flip; any
     failed step soft-deletes the sale. Audit 'metal_sale_create'.
     DB smoke (rolled back): sale S-xxx kind=metal, 1 line, cash 400 +
     transfer 230, locked=true, and the lock trigger refused a total
     change.

 437. SELL RATES ON /inventory/spot-prices. Each (metal, purity) card
     in "Pay and sell rates" now has two forms — Pay rate % and Sell

 421. PHASE 2 SCOPE + MONEY MODEL (brief). A quote builder for custom
     work: The piece → The price → The payment. Decision that shapes
     everything: every price block is a LINE with a COST (what the shop
     pays out — metal at spot, a wax print, a stone's cost basis,
     outsourced labor) and a PRICE (what the customer pays); the quote
     margin is (Σprice − Σcost)/Σprice and "you keep" is Σprice − Σcost.
     A target margin fills price = cost/(1−m) for every cost-bearing
     line staff did not type by hand (typing LOCKS a line; pure-price
     lines like bench labor or an engraving fee count fully toward
     "keep"). Because pure-price lines add to price but not cost, the
     quote margin lands at or above the target — but a locked low price
     can drag it under the manager's MINIMUM, and both the footer and
     the server say so (saving is refused). Fractions everywhere (0.5 =
     50%), matching the settings columns and DB CHECKs.
     lib/custom-quotes/math.ts + 17 tests (lineTotals, quoteTotals,
     priceFromCost clamped at 95%, applyTargetMargin, depositSplit,
     laborPrice, expiryDate, metalLabel).

 422. MIGRATIONS (APPLIED). 0062 adds message_kind 'custom_quote_link'
     alone (enum-value-in-own-transaction rule). 0063: enums
     custom_quote_status (draft/sent/accepted/declined/expired/
     converted/voided), custom_piece_category (ring…nameplate, grillz,
     other), custom_quote_line_kind (metal/wax/labor/stone/setting/
     extra); settings columns custom_quote_default_margin_pct 0.50,
     _min_margin_pct 0.30, _default_deposit_pct 0.50, _expiry_days 14,
     _labor_rate 75, _show_cost_breakdown FALSE; custom_quotes
     (CQ-000001 via next_tenant_counter 'custom_quote', public_token
     from repair_public_token(), frozen spot summary columns, deposit /
     balance, expires_on, repair_ticket_id) + custom_quote_lines (qty,
     unit_cost, unit_price, totals, price_locked, details jsonb,
     inventory_item_id). FREEZE: trg_custom_quotes_lock refuses any
     change to the piece or money columns once status leaves draft/sent
     (status, timestamps, ticket link, token, notes still move — "void
     and requote", same spirit as rules 2/14); lines follow the parent.
     RLS mirrors repair_tickets. RPC custom_quote_public(token) →
     customer-safe jsonb, NEVER a cost, lines only when the breakdown
     setting is on (label/qty/price). Templates for the new kind seeded
     per tenant + tenant trigger. DB SMOKE in one rolled-back
     transaction (results carried out via RAISE): CQ-000001 assigned,
     32-char token, public doc keys contain no cost/notes, update of
     price_total on an accepted quote → custom_quote_locked, line
     insert/delete on it → locked, status→converted still allowed.

 428. GOTCHA — JETBRAINS MONO LIGATURES CRASH FONTKIT. The repair PDF
     500'd ("RangeError: Offset is outside the bounds of the DataView"
     in DataView.getInt16 inside renderToBuffer) while the quote PDF
     rendered fine. A throwaway probe bisected it to the tracking URL
     rendered in JetBrains Mono: the mono WOFF ships programming
     ligatures ("//", "://"), and fontkit's GSUB pass over them throws.
     Plain words, digits, colons, slashes alone all render; "//" does
     not. URLs now render in Inter; lib/pdf/shared.ts documents the
     rule (mono for numerals only). The probe script was deleted after
     use.

 429. GOTCHA — `npm run build` FAILED WITH "next/font/google queries have
     exactly one entry" (8 Turbopack errors) when run CONCURRENTLY with
     `npm run lint` + vitest. Not item 400's npm-install case; the same
     build passed unchanged when run alone right after. Treat a
     font-import-map build error as "something else was compiling" and
     retry alone before deleting .next.

 430. BROWSER SMOKE EXTENDED. scripts/smoke-browser.mjs gained
     /inventory/spot-prices, /repair/board, /settings/repair-stages,
     /repair/quotes, /repair/quotes/new, /repair/quotes/[id] + /edit,
     /settings/custom-quotes and the two PDF routes (Chrome renders
     them; the harness sees status + console). Cold dev server: 36/36
     clean except /consignors/new → /consignors, which is the
     consignment module gate (run with --gates), not a regression. The
     quote detail / edit / PDF routes ran against a temporary draft
     quote inserted by SQL and hard-deleted afterwards (a draft quote is
     pre-sale data, not a posted financial record).

 431. [CRITICAL] EVERY PDF ROUTE WAS BROKEN IN PRODUCTION — fonts not
     traced. Eddy printed the first real quote on pawn-three.vercel.app
     and got "render failed"; the same quote rendered 200 locally through
     the authenticated harness, so it was environment, not code.
     lib/pdf/fonts.ts reads the @fontsource woff files from node_modules
     with fs.readFileSync at RUNTIME (deliberately — Turbopack cannot
     bundle them as imports), so Next's file tracer never saw them and
     the serverless bundle shipped without them: the route's .nft.json
     had 0 fontsource entries. That means buy / loan / sale / appraisal /
     all seven report PDFs had ALSO been 500ing on Vercel the whole time
     (nobody printed one in prod, or nobody reported it). Fix:
     next.config.ts outputFileTracingIncludes for /api/print/**,
     /api/appraisals/**, /api/reports/** → the four woff files (route-
     glob keys per Next 16 docs, values relative to the project root).
     Verified after build: 8 fontsource entries in each PDF route trace.
     Lesson: anything read with fs at runtime from node_modules needs a
     tracing include, and "works locally, render failed in prod" for a
     PDF is this until proven otherwise.

 PHASE 1 (items 411-420 below).

 411. PHASE 1 SCOPE (brief): a public, read-only link per repair ticket
     that staff text or WhatsApp to the customer so "is my piece ready?"
     stops being a phone call. Shows store name/logo, ticket number, the
     piece, a reference photo, promised date, a progress bar of NAMED
     STAGES (configurable per service type), quoted / paid / balance and
     the store phone — never the customer's identity, address, other
     tickets or internal notes. Everything below is on main.

 412. PUBLIC PAGE /t/<token> (src/app/(public)/t/[token]/). The token is
     the ONLY input (rule 8: the tenant comes from the token). page.tsx
     validates the shape (32 hex), calls the SECURITY DEFINER RPC
     repair_public_ticket(p_token) through the ANON SSR client (granted
     to anon; no table policy opened), then lib/repair/public-ticket.ts
     shapePublicTicket() REBUILDS the document key-by-key from an
     allowlist so a future "helpful" column in the RPC cannot leak;
     public-ticket.test.ts feeds a deliberately leaky document and
     asserts the forbidden keys (customer_id, customer_*, first/last
     name, email, address, notes_internal, description, assigned_to,
     pickup_signature_path, public_token, …) and the customer's actual
     values are absent. The reference photo (kind reference, else
     intake; never the pickup signature) leaves the RPC as a storage
     PATH and is signed for 1h with the service role (rule 12). The RPC
     returns NULL — one generic not-found — for unknown / revoked token,
     deleted or voided ticket, inactive tenant. robots noindex. Opens in
     the CUSTOMER's language (customers.language is the one customer
     field that crosses) with the EN|ES toggle. Verified against the
     built app: 200 + full content for RT-000001's token, HTML free of
     the customer's name/phone/email/ids; 404 for an unknown and a
     malformed token; Spanish via Accept-Language.

 413. STAGES — patches/0061 repair_stages (tenant_id, service_type,
     sort_order, name_en, name_es, percent 0-100, maps_to_statuses
     repair_status[], is_active; UNIQUE(tenant, type, sort_order); RLS
     staff read / owner-chain_admin-manager write, mirroring
     tenant_loan_rates). seed_default_repair_stages(tenant, type?,
     reset?) loads the brief's defaults — Repair/Sizing etc.: Received →
     Diagnosed/Quoted → Approved → In progress → Quality check → Ready
     for pickup → Picked up; Custom: Design → Design approved → Wax/3D
     print → Casting → Metal work → Stone setting → Finishing → Quality
     check → Ready for pickup → Delivered — for every existing tenant
     and, via trg_tenant_after_insert_seed_repair_tracking, every new
     one. repair_tickets gained public_token (nullable = revoked),
     public_token_created_at, public_stage_id (FK, ON DELETE SET NULL),
     public_stage_updated_at. STAGE ↔ STATUS IS THE DATABASE'S JOB: the
     BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF status trigger
     repair_tickets_sync_public_stage moves the pointer to the stage
     whose maps_to_statuses contains the new status — first match going
     forward, last match going back — UNLESS the current stage already
     covers that status, so a custom piece sitting at "Casting" is not
     yanked back to "Wax" by a timer-driven in_progress re-entry. That
     one trigger covers the 20+ detail actions and the board drag
     without any of them knowing. Hand-advanced sub-stages move via
     setPublicStageAction ('next' or a stage id) — one tap on the board
     card footer (StageQuickRow, rendered OUTSIDE the draggable <button>
     because a button cannot nest a button) and on the ticket's
     TrackingPanel (Next stage + a picker). Backfill gave the existing
     ticket a token and a stage (UPDATE … SET status = status fires an
     UPDATE OF status trigger even when unchanged).

 414. STAFF UI (components/repair/TrackingPanel.tsx on /repair/[id]):
     the link (selectable), Copy (clipboard), Open, Send by SMS, New
     link (confirm; old one dies immediately), Revoke (confirm; page →
     not-found until regenerated); the stage name + % bar + Next stage +
     picker; link to Settings → Repair stages. All actions in
     src/app/(staff)/repair/stage-actions.ts (shared with the board),
     any pawn/repair staff role; every one writes audit_log
     (repair_tracking_link_send / _regenerate / _revoke,
     repair_stage_set — the token itself is never logged) and a
     repair_ticket_events 'note' row so the timeline shows it.

 415. SEND BY SMS — new message_kind 'repair_tracking_link' (patches/
     0060, its own apply because Postgres cannot use a new enum value in
     the transaction that adds it — same split as 0049/0050, 0052/0053).
     0061's seed_repair_tracking_templates() seeds EN/ES SMS + email
     (WhatsApp disabled until a Content SID exists) for every tenant and
     new tenants; registered in messageKindSchema, the comms settings
     KIND_ORDER, SendReminderDialog and i18n kindLabels/Descriptions.
     sendTrackingLinkAction goes through dispatchMessage with
     channelOverride 'sms' (the button says SMS; a WhatsApp-preferring
     customer still gets a text), so tenant Twilio creds, customer
     language, message_log and the STOP footer all come from
     /settings/communications as the brief required. Dispatcher failure
     reasons (no_destination, opted_out_preference, template_missing,
     template_disabled, provider_failed) are translated in
     t.repair.tracking.errors.

 416. {{tracking_link}} IN THE READY TEXTS. 0061 appends " Track it
     here: {{tracking_link}}" / " Siga su reparación: {{tracking_link}}"
     to every repair_ready and repair_pickup_reminder template body that
     lacks it (operator edits otherwise preserved; verified 6/6 rows).
     The repair-ready-reminders cron now passes tracking_link ('' when
     revoked). NOTE this cron — like every cron — had NEVER fired in

 417. AUTO-TEXT WHEN READY (optional setting) — settings.
     repair_ready_auto_notify (default FALSE). lib/repair/notify.ts
     notifyRepairReadyIfEnabled() runs after markCompleteAction,
     approveQaAction and the board move to 'ready': sends repair_ready
     (with tracking_link) immediately, skips if a repair_ready row
     already exists in message_log for the ticket, never throws (a
     comms hiccup must not undo a status change). The daily cron stays
     the catch-up and already skips tickets that got the ready text.
     Toggle lives on /settings/repair-stages.

 418. /settings/repair-stages (owner / chain_admin / manager; has_repair
     gate; hub card on /settings when the repair module is on). Tabs per
     service type; rows: EN name, ES name, %, auto-advance statuses
     (chips; 'voided' deliberately not mappable — a voided ticket's page
     is a not-found), active, reorder, remove, add; Save; Reset to
     defaults (confirm → seed_default_repair_stages(…, reset=TRUE)).
     saveRepairStagesAction renumbers in TWO passes (park survivors on
     negative sort_order, then final (i+1)*10) because the UNIQUE
     (tenant, type, sort_order) index would bounce a swap mid-way;
     deletes removed rows (ticket pointers go NULL via the FK) and
     re-points those tickets to the first active stage mapping their
     status — the same rule the trigger applies when a ticket has no
     usable stage. Audit: repair_stages_update / _reset /
     repair_notify_setting.

 419. PURE HELPERS + TESTS: lib/repair/stages.ts (nextStage, stageIndex,
     progressPercent clamp, stageName, isValidPublicToken, trackingUrl —
     /t/<token>, never a ticket number or customer id in the URL) with

     duplicate logic consolidated); detectLanguageFromHeaders() moved
     from the (public) layout into lib/i18n/server.ts for the same

 420. GOTCHA — 'use client' not-found.tsx UNDER A DYNAMIC SEGMENT. The
     first /t/[token]/not-found.tsx was a client component (useI18n for
     the bilingual copy). In the PRODUCTION build, notFound() from the
     page rendered Next's DEFAULT "404: This page could not be found"
     instead — no error in the server log, tsc/lint/build all green —
     while the sibling /s/[slug]/not-found.tsx (a server component)
     worked. Rewritten as a server component that reads Accept-Language
     via detectLanguageFromHeaders() + getDictionary(); custom 404 then
     rendered in EN and ES. Next's bundled docs (not-found.md) note that
     client hooks in not-found need client-side data fetching — treat
     not-found.tsx as server-only in this repo.

 PHASE 0 (items 401-410 below).



 401. CAMERA / MICROPHONE ERRORS CLASSIFIED (commit 3407dd1, 2026-09-24,
     shipped between checkpoints and not logged until now). Eddy hit
     "Could not access camera. Check browser permissions.
     (NotReadableError)" — the one getUserMedia error that is NOT about
     permissions (device busy in another app). src/lib/media/errors.ts
     classifies the DOMException into denied / inUse / notFound /
     constraints / insecure / unknown with matching EN+ES advice; the
     mic path previously swallowed the error in `catch {}` and always
     said "denied". 18 tests pin NotReadableError → inUse.

 402. [CRITICAL] SPOT PRICES FROZEN SINCE 2026-04-30 — ROOT CAUSE. Brief
     item 0.1. Every melt value and buy payout had been computed from
     24K = $148.52/g for six months while the market was ~$126/g (shop
     overpaying ~18%). Two independent failures, found by read-only
     diagnosis before touching code:
       (a) Stooq's quote endpoint (`stooq.com/q/l/?s=xauusd&f=sd2t2c&h&
           e=csv`) returns HTTP 404 for EVERY symbol and host variant,
           and its /q/d/l/ history endpoint now sits behind a JavaScript
           proof-of-work challenge. Eight manual "Refresh now" attempts
           failed with "No quotes returned from Stooq" (last
           2026-07-01); the failure was only visible as a one-line echo
           under the button and as audit rows nobody looks at.
       (b) The cron NEVER ran once: zero spot_price_refresh audit rows
           with tenant_id NULL in the whole table. CRON_SECRET was not
           set in Vercel Production, so /api/cron/refresh-spot-prices
           returned 401 before any logging — the same unset secret
           Progress.txt had carried as IMMEDIATE for several sessions.
     Verified live providers the same day: gold-api.com (free, no key,
     spot XAU/XAG/XPT/XPD), Yahoo v8 chart futures GC=F/SI=F/PL=F/PA=F
     (contango caveat), Yahoo XAUUSD=X delisted, goldprice.org 403,
     metals.dev / goldapi.io need keys.

 403. PROVIDER FALLBACK CHAIN (src/lib/spot-prices/sources/). A
     SpotSource interface ({id, label, fetch({fetchedAt})} that NEVER
     throws) with three implementations: gold-api.ts (primary, spot),
     yahoo-futures.ts (fallback, futures — documented why it is not
     primary), stooq.ts (last resort, documented as 404 since 2026-11).
     http.ts is the one shared fetch (8s timeout, no-store, UA, string
     errors). index.ts `fetchSpotQuotesWithFallback(sources)` walks the
     chain: first provider returning ALL FOUR metals wins; otherwise
     gaps fill metal-by-metal from whichever provider had that metal
     first, so one flaky symbol cannot freeze the feed. Each SpotQuote
     carries its provider → spot_prices.source is now per-row truth
     ('gold-api' | 'yahoo-futures' | 'stooq'). 10 unit tests on the
     chain + parsers (sources accept an injected list; no network in
     tests).

 404. FETCH LOG (patches/0058, APPLIED). New platform-wide table
     spot_price_fetches: trigger ('cron'|'manual'), triggered_by,
     tenant_id (attribution only), started/finished, ok, source, quotes,
     attempted, inserted, error, attempts JSONB (one entry per provider
     tried: source, ok, quotes, ms, error). RLS enabled; ONE policy,
     spot_price_fetches_read_all FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() IS NOT
     NULL) — an exact mirror of spot_prices_read_all (0013); no write
     policy, service role only. refresh.ts writes a row on EVERY run,
     success or failure, in a non-fatal `finish()` (a logging failure
     can never break a refresh). Spot Prices page gained a "Refresh
     history" table (last 20) showing the provider trail, e.g.
     "stooq ✗ (gold: HTTP 404 …) 812ms → yahoo-futures ✓ 320ms".

 405. refresh.ts REWRITTEN around the chain. refreshSpotPrices({trigger,
     userId?, tenantId?}) → RefreshSummary {ok, source, quotes,
     attempted, inserted, attempts, error?, fetchLogId}. Partial
     coverage (<4 metals) is ok:true with an explanatory error string.
     Cron route mirrors the summary into audit_log (record_id =
     fetchLogId) and now logs LOUDLY when CRON_SECRET is unset instead of
     silently 401ing. scripts/refresh-spot-prices.ts is a CLI for the
     same path (`npx tsx --env-file=.env.local scripts/refresh-spot-
     prices.ts`). Verified: real run → gold-api, 4 quotes, 10/10 rows,
     24K $126.0920/g ($3,921.90/oz), 18K $94.5690/g; then the built app
     on :3061 with a test CRON_SECRET → no header 401, wrong secret 401,
     right secret 200 + another real refresh + fetch-log row.

 406. STALE-PRICE GUARD (24h). src/lib/spot-prices/staleness.ts is pure
     (SPOT_STALE_AFTER_HOURS=24, assessSpotFreshness, newestFetchedAt;
     no price at all = stale; 6 tests). lookup.getSpotFreshness() reads
     the newest fetched_at (cached, fail-CLOSED on read error).
     components/inventory/StalePriceBanner.tsx renders a red danger
     banner on Spot Prices, Buy Outright, New Pawn Ticket and the Loan
     Calculator; StaleSpotConfirm renders the manager checkbox
     (name=stale_spot_ack). stale-gate.ts is the server half:
     checkStaleSpotGate({formData, tenantRole, involvesMetal}) — holds
     the submission only when prices are stale AND the items price
     precious metal (meltMetalFromItem != null; a loan on a laptop
     passes), requires role ∈ owner/manager/chain_admin AND the ack
     field, returns translated codes stale_spot_price_confirm_required /
     stale_spot_price_manager_required (wired into ruleErrorText via
     t.spotPrices.errors). Buy form blocks submit client-side (it knows
     per-row metal); pawn form shows the confirm and lets the server
     decide (it only gets collateral counts). Both audit rows record
     stale_spot_ack {confirmed_by, spot_fetched_at, spot_age_hours}.
     No PIN system exists, so "manager confirms" = a manager-role
     session ticks the box; a clerk is told to get a manager to refresh.

 407. 18K "×0.6000" EXPLAINED AND FIXED (brief 0.2). Not a purity bug:
     18K spot was exactly 0.75 × 24K (correct). The badge was tenant

 408. DEVELOPER TEXT ON /pawn/new (brief 0.3). "Default rate seeded by

--- Session 54 (2026-09-23) — ID scan in the customer header + HEIC uploads fixed end to end ---

 393. ID SCAN IN THE CUSTOMER HEADER
     (src/app/(staff)/customers/[id]/content.tsx, IdScanBlock). Eddy asked
     for the ID to be visible immediately, marking the empty band right of
     the customer name. Built as a THUMBNAIL that opens the full scan in a
     new tab, NOT a banner filling the band: DESIGN-lunaazul.md says ID
     scans are "never rendered at hero scale, never shown in any browse
     surface". A customer's own detail page is the record itself rather
     than a browse surface, so a small gated tile is consistent; a
     full-bleed driver's license on a shop-floor monitor is not. Reuses
     the 1h signed URL the Documents section already builds (rule 12) —
     no new plumbing, bucket stays private. Renders an explicit "No ID on
     file" tile rather than blank space, because for a pawn a missing ID
     is the thing staff most need to notice.

 394. [BUG] HEIC UPLOADS REJECTED EVERYWHERE — THE ALLOWLIST WAS NEVER THE
     PROBLEM. 'image/heic' was already in all seven ALLOWED_*_MIME_TYPES
     lists. The bug was that every check read the BROWSER-DECLARED type:
         ALLOWED_DOCUMENT_MIME_TYPES.includes(file.type)
     ROOT CAUSE: file.type comes from the OPERATING SYSTEM's type
     registry, not from the file. Windows has no registered MIME for HEIC
     unless the HEIF Image Extensions are installed, so Chrome hands the
     server file.type === '' for a valid IMG_1234.HEIC straight off an
     iPhone. Empty string is not in the allowlist -> mime_not_allowed,
     while the format sat in the allowlist the whole time. Some sources
     report 'image/heif' instead, which also missed.
     FIX: src/lib/uploads/mime.ts resolveUploadMime() — believe file.type
     when recognised, else fall back to the filename extension, and
     normalise aliases (image/heif, image/jpg, *-sequence). The resolved
     value is also what gets STORED, so files uploaded with a blank type
     no longer land in the bucket labelled Content-Type ''.
     Applied at ALL 11 call sites, not just the customer ID scan Eddy hit:
     the identical check was silently rejecting iPhone photos on pawn
     collateral, buy outright, repair intake, repair pickup signatures,
     appraisals and inventory.
     Still an allowlist gate — callers reject anything not on their list,
     and neither an extension nor a declared type proves file contents.
     Private buckets + signed URLs remain what actually contains a
     mislabelled upload.

 395. [BUG] THE FILE PICKER WAS HIDING .heic BEFORE IT COULD BE CHOSEN.
     accept="" listed MIME types only, and on Windows the picker maps
     those through the same registry, so .heic files were greyed out.
     Added explicit extensions across all 10 upload forms.

 396. HEIC CONVERTED TO JPEG AT UPLOAD (src/lib/uploads/convert.ts).
     After 394 the file uploaded but STILL could not be seen — Chrome,
     Edge and Firefox refuse to paint HEIC (only Safari renders it), so
     an iPhone ID scan was a file staff could download and never view,
     which defeats item 393 entirely. prepareUpload() transcodes HEIC to
     JPEG via heic-convert (pure JS, no native binaries, runs on Vercel)
     BEFORE the bytes reach storage. Measured on the real 449KB scan
     already in the bucket: 429ms, 144MB peak RSS, 509KB out at quality
     0.85, 2557x1601 preserved.
     Converting on the way IN, not on every view: it happens once per
     file, and the stored bytes and the recorded mime_type stay honest
     about each other, so every downstream reader (header tile, documents
     list, ticket PDFs, portal) just sees a JPEG.
     DELIBERATE FAILURE MODE: on any conversion error the ORIGINAL file is
     stored rather than throwing. Losing a pawn's ID scan because a
     decoder tripped is worse than storing a file the browser cannot
     inline — the record still exists for compliance, the download still
     works, and mime_type still describes the real bytes. Files over 20MB
     skip conversion rather than risk the function's memory. byte_size now
     records the STORED length, not the input length.

 397. isBrowserRenderableImage() — HEIC passes startsWith('image/') but no
     mainstream browser paints it, so item 393's tile would have rendered
     a broken image on the shop's own machines. Lives in mime.ts (not
     convert.ts) precisely so a CLIENT component can ask the question
     without dragging the Node-only decoder into the bundle.

 398. BACKFILLED THE ONE HEIC ALREADY IN THE BUCKET. Non-destructive:
     wrote the .jpg, repointed customer_documents.storage_path +
     mime_type + byte_size, LEFT the original .heic object in place.
     Verified in a real browser — img.naturalWidth is 2557, i.e. Chrome
     genuinely decoded it; rendered 185x117 in the header tile, zero
     console errors.

 399. PRE-SAVE GATE — SEVEN COPIES OF pickExt CONSOLIDATED. Six upload
     actions plus lib/supabase/storage.ts each carried a private
     pickExt/pickExtension with identical filename-first logic, differing
     only in which types the fallback covered (appraisals omitted pdf;
     only storage.ts knew gif). Now one extensionForUpload() in
     lib/uploads/mime.ts with the SUPERSET map, so the only behaviour
     change is that a file with no usable extension and a pdf/gif type
     gets 'pdf'/'gif' instead of 'bin'. Pinned by tests so a future edit
     cannot quietly change storage paths. 538 tests, green build.

 400. GOTCHA WORTH REMEMBERING: npm install MID-SESSION desynced
     Turbopack's dev module graph and every route started 500ing on
     `[next]/internal/font/google/dm_sans_*.module.css — Can't resolve
     '@vercel/turbopack-next/internal/font/google/font'`. It looked like
     the app had been broken by the upload refactor; it had not. The
     production build was green throughout, which is the fastest way to
     tell the two apart. Cure: stop the dev server BY PID, delete .next,
     restart. First cold run after an install may still show it on a
     route or two before settling.

--- Session 53 (2026-09-22) — Customer physical-description dropdowns + race wired end to end ---

 387. DESCRIPTION FIELDS ARE DROPDOWNS NOW (sex / hair_color / eye_color /
     race). FL Stat. § 539.001 requires a physical description of the
     pledgor, but these were free-text boxes, so the same person could be
     'Brown', 'brown' or 'BROWN' depending on who wrote the ticket — and
     the police-report export would inherit the mess. Converted on ALL
     THREE customer surfaces, not just the two Eddy named:
       - /customers/new          (add)
       - /customers/[id]         (edit)
       - QuickCustomerModal      (inline add during pawn intake — same
                                  free-text fields, would have stayed
                                  inconsistent)
     The first two share src/components/customers/CustomerFormFields.tsx,
     so one change covered both.

 388. race WAS DEAD WEIGHT — NOW WIRED END TO END (closes old backlog 8).
     The column has existed since patch 0047 and the xPawn import fills
     it, but it was in NO read query, NO form, NO validator and NEITHER
     write path — so imported values were invisible and unreachable from
     the UI. Added to: the [id]/page.tsx select list (without this the
     dropdown would load blank and blank the column on save), the shared
     form type + EMPTY, both echo-rebuild paths, customerSchema,
     lib/customers/create.ts and customers/[id]/actions.ts.

 389. THE OPTION VALUES ARE THE DATA'S OWN VOCABULARY — the real decision
     here. Audited all 5,218 customers BEFORE choosing. They already
     carry a vocabulary from xPawn: Title-case English words ('Black',
     'Red Or Auburn', 'Hispanic'), NOT NCIC three-letter codes
     (BLK/BRO/HAZ). So src/lib/customers/physical-description.ts
     reproduces that vocabulary verbatim, casing included, and the
     dropdowns select data that is already there. Choosing NCIC codes
     would have split the column into two vocabularies and forced every
     future reader — starting with the LeadsOnline exporter, which does
     not read these fields yet — to understand both forever.
     Observed distribution: sex M 2944 / F 2262 / 'm' 1; hair 16 distinct
     (Black 2023, Brown 1921, Grey 441, Blonde 405, Bald 194 …); eyes 9
     distinct; race 8 distinct (Hispanic 3691, White 857, Black 616 …).

 390. OFF-LIST VALUES ARE PRESERVED — the correctness property that makes
     this safe. descriptionOptions() appends any stored value that is not
     a canonical option as its own option and preselects it, so editing
     an unrelated field (a phone number) can NEVER silently blank a field
     the statute requires the shop to hold. race also stays free text in
     Zod for the same reason — a strict enum would reject an unrelated
     edit on any legacy row.
     Verified in a real browser against the record that actually has the
     problem (Eddy's own: sex 'm', hair_color 'BLACK'): the edit form
     renders 6 and 17 options instead of 5 and 16, both preselected, and
     all four fields round-trip their stored value. The add form shows
     the clean lists (5 / 16 / 11 / 9 including the blank).

 391. PRE-SAVE GATE FINDINGS, fixed in the same checkpoint.
     (a) DUPLICATE LOGIC: the four <select> blocks in QuickCustomerModal
         repeated the same options-map. Extracted a local
         DescriptionSelect component. Behaviour-preserving, and verified
         by driving the modal in a real browser (it only mounts on click,
         so the route-level smoke never reaches it) — 5/16/11/9 options,
         zero console errors.
     (b) i18n BLIND SPOT: descriptionOptions falls back to the raw value
         when a label is missing, so a half-translated locale renders
         'Pacific Islander' in Spanish instead of failing — invisible to
         tsc AND to a screenshot. Added tests asserting every option key
         has a non-blank label in EVERY locale and that no dictionary
         carries a label for an option that no longer exists.
     523 tests total (26 new). tsc + lint clean.

 392. patches/0057-normalize-customer-description-values.sql WRITTEN BUT
     NOT APPLIED — awaiting Eddy's go-ahead, because it UPDATEs customer
     records the statute requires the shop to hold. It normalizes the 3
     casing-variant rows ('m' -> 'M', 'BLACK'/'black' -> 'Black').
     Nothing is broken without it: those rows display and save correctly
     via the preserve-off-list rule; the patch only stops 'BLACK' sitting
     next to 'Black' in the menu like two different colors.
     DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED: hair 'Auburn' (36 rows) vs 'Red Or Auburn'
     (87 rows). Both are offered as separate options. Merging them is a
     judgement call about what the shop means, not a casing fix.
